 The job operator (`lib/job-operator.sh`, headless `claude -p` with `templates/job-operator-prompt.md`, read-only tools) diagnoses from the rows, logs and owner-repo code, writes `handoff/job-incident-<job>-<date>.md` in `~/.walkaway-cli/job-fixes/<job>-<date>/`, and then either renders a template-v2 fix plan (`## Repo` = owner repo) that is queued/launched like any walkaway build (one per job per day, one builder per repo), or escalates ONE question when the cause or fix touches a PROTECTED action, needs the job's live action on a protected job, or is not high-confidence. After the fix merges it runs the job's safe retry path once (`retry.command`; only ShowingTime has one) and the next check confirms OK. The 09/13/17 heartbeat lists every non-OK job whose window has opened; `walkaway status` prints a JOBS table under the builds.
 
+## Data-series gap watching (data/series.yaml)
+
+```bash
+./install-supervisor.sh --data-on    # touch ~/.walkaway-cli/data.enabled (--data-off removes it)
+bash lib/data.sh list                # every scheduled pull/cron: watched series + excluded entries (with reason)
+bash lib/data.sh check bb-showings-daily [--as-of "2026-11-09 14:00"]   # read-only per-period row counts (JSON)
+bash lib/data.sh dump                # every entry's check (AC1 evidence); floors re-derives every floor from real rows
+bash lib/data.sh state <series>      # one series' gap state
+```
+
+`data/series.yaml` lists every scheduled pull, cron and agent in Showrunner (audrey-ops-platform) and Throughline (throughline-app). There are 49 entries: 21 watched dated series and 28 excluded with a reason, such as sends, actions already covered by jobs.yaml, inbox scans and repair crons.
+
+Each watched series names:
+- the table and its key column (date, ISO yearweek, or a timestamp range);
+- its filters and cadence (daily, optionally `days:` weekdays; weekly; monthly);
+- `lag_days` and `ready_by`: when the period's own pull and retries are done;
+- a floor (rows per period, from real 30-day medians);
+- whether the source can re-pull a past period at all;
+- its existing re-pull paths;
+- the `fix_hook` contract, a date-range hook a fix build adds when no path exists.
+
+Counts are read-only GETs with `Prefer: count=exact`; the service-role key is read at call time and passed to curl on stdin. Complete periods are cached and re-counted once a day.
+
+While data watching is on, every supervisor tick moves each gap (series, period) through these states:
+
+| State | Meaning | ntfy |
+|---|---|---|
+| OPEN | rows missing or under the floor after the period's ready_by (14-day / 4-week / 2-month lookback) | one line per series per tick, dates grouped |
+| REPULLING | the series' own re-pull fired for exactly the gap dates (or a self-heal wait started); verified at verify_at | - |
+| CLOSED | a count confirmed rows at/above the floor (rows before -> after, and via what) | one line |
+| FIXING | no re-pull path covers the gap, or the re-pull failed twice: the data operator queued/launched a template-v2 fix build | one line |
+| ESCALATED | one question for Jim: the dates are lost (the source cannot re-pull), a post-fix re-pull failed, or the cause is protected or unclear | one line, priority high |
+
+Re-pull paths are the series' own. Writing the run-now flag or calling a cron route is the same thing the app's own pull does.
+- `run_now`: the Showrunner run-now flag `extension_report_state.run_now_requested_at`, for one allow-listed report type.
+- `http`: the route's own cron GET with CRON_SECRET (meta-sync, pulse-daily-sync?ifMissing=1, pulse-macro, pulse-events-refresh, pulse-signals, pulse-market-health).
+- `command`: the ST URL trigger, which re-pulls D-1..D-8 and BrokerBay yesterday.
+- `self_heal`: the extension heals tracking/spc at today-8..today-2; connect, markable and TMA refill their own windows.
+
+Limits on every re-pull:
+- Amazon re-pulls only run in business hours (09:00-19:00), never while a CC batch is in flight, and at most one per 15 min across all series.
+- A gap gets at most 2 tries per day. A guard deferral does not count as a try.
+
+The data operator (`lib/data-operator.sh`, headless `claude -p` with `templates/data-operator-prompt.md`, read-only) works like the job operator:
+- It writes `job-incident-<series>-<date>.md`.
+- It then renders a fix plan whose `## Date-range re-pull hook` quotes the series' `fix_hook` verbatim, or it asks ONE question. Lost dates and post-fix failures are escalated without a model call.
+- One fix build per series per day; one builder per repo.
+
+After the fix merges, the gaps go back to OPEN and the hook (`requires_fix` in series.yaml) re-pulls exactly those dates. The count then confirms CLOSED. The 09/13/17 heartbeat lists only series with open gaps. `walkaway status` prints a DATA table (series, last complete, open gaps, last action) under JOBS.
+

+~/.walkaway-cli/data.enabled                   Data watching on (flag file)

+~/.walkaway-cli/data-fixes/<series>-<date>/handoff  Incident, evidence JSON, generated fix plan
+~/.walkaway-cli/logs/data-repull.log           Every re-pull trigger with the rows before
+~/.walkaway-cli/logs/data-operator-<series>.log Data operator runs
 ```

+# Dated data series the walkaway supervisor watches for gaps (lib/data.sh). One entry per scheduled pull / cron /
+# agent found in both owner repos (supervisor-v3-data Phase 0: handoff/supervisor-v3-data-evidence/phase0-inventory.md).
+# Watched entries get gap detection + re-pull; excluded entries (watch: false) say why and, where a table exists,
+# carry a read-only probe so the dump proves the source is real. Times are local (America/New_York, the Mini).
+#
+#   name        series id (state files, incidents, ntfy lines)
+#   watch       true = gap detection on; false = listed only (reason + covered_by)
+#   owner_repo  repo that fix builds for this series run in
+#   source      Supabase project (sources below); reads are GETs, the key is read from env_file at call time
+#   table/key   key.column holds the period: type date (YYYY-MM-DD), yearweek (ISO YYYYWW int) or timestamp
+#               (rows counted inside the period's local-time range)
+#   filters     extra PostgREST filters (placeholders: {audrey_user}, {pulse_team})
+#   cadence     daily | weekly (period anchor = Monday) | monthly (anchor = the 1st)
+#   days        optional, daily only: weekdays the series is expected (mon..sun, e.g. [mon, tue, wed, thu, fri]);
+#               omitted = every day. Phase 0 found no weekday-only pull in either repo (all run daily incl. weekends)
+#   lag_days    days after the period anchor the data lands; ready_by = local time that day it must be complete.
+#               Before (anchor + lag_days) at ready_by the series' own pull/retries still own the period: no GAP.
+#   floor       rows per period at or above which the period is complete (Phase 0 formula: a constant count is
+#               structural, otherwise 50% of the 30-day / 8-week median); floor_basis says which
+#   historical  yes | partial | no: can the SOURCE re-pull a past period at all ("no" = a gap is lost data)
+#   repull      existing re-pull paths, tried in order; each covers `ages` (days/weeks/months before the current
+#               period) and optional local `hours` [start, end). Methods:
+#                 run_now   Showrunner run-now: writes extension_report_state.run_now_requested_at = now for one base
+#                           report_type (the column the run-now route sets; the extension pulls on its next poll)
+#                 http      GET {app_url}{path} with Bearer <cron_secret_var> (the route's own cron call)
+#                 command   a fixed local command (e.g. the ST URL trigger the login agent opens)
+#                 self_heal nothing to trigger: the series refills by itself; the try is the wait
+#               guard: amazon = business hours + no CC batch in flight + paced (guards.amazon); any other name =
+#               a guards.<name> read-only query that must return a row before the trigger fires (st_brief_sent)
+#               requires_fix: true = the hook a fix build adds (contract below); used only after that fix merged.
+#               {from}/{to} (command or http path) = the first and last DAY of the gap range: a weekly gap is
+#               Monday..Sunday, a monthly gap the 1st..the last day
+#   fix_hook    the date-range re-pull hook a fix build must add when no existing path covers a gap (the plan's
+#               "date-range run-now hooks"), stated as the exact trigger the supervisor will call after the merge
+
+defaults:
+  lookback_days: 14          # daily periods checked: the last 14 days
+  lookback_weeks: 4
+  lookback_months: 2
+  max_tries_per_day: 2       # re-pull tries per gap per local day
+  verify_after_mins: 30
+
+vars:
+  audrey_user: d3a83609-87a8-46aa-aa74-a2105d275748   # jobs.yaml vars.audrey_user
+  pulse_team: d14dce4f-9111-45e7-a52d-18d5f6dd04b6    # jobs.yaml vars.pulse_team
+

+  throughline:

+    key_var: SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY

+guards:

+      predicate: 'any(.[]; (.status == "PLANNED" or .status == "RUNNING") or any((.cc_optin_batches // [])[]; .status == "SUBMITTED"))'

+    # The ST daily ingest sends the day's Pulse brief when it lands yesterday's total and the brief has not gone

+    source: throughline

+    wait_reason: "today's Pulse brief not sent yet (an ST re-pull would send it)"

 # Walkaway Build Plan: supervisor-v3-data
 
+## RESUME DIRECTIVE (auto, approved fix 1, 2026-11-15 12:51 ET) - read first
+RESUME DIRECTIVE (operator, Codex cycle 1 P1 + P2), approved as proposed in failure-report-supervisor-v3-data-codex.md, with these additions.
+
+What failed: Codex cycle 1 at 275c4b9 found two real problems.
+- P1: fix_hook contracts have no executable requires_fix re-pull method, and http paths don't get {from}/{to}.
+- P2: a verify uses up a try even when this tick's count errored.
+
+Fix to apply:
+1. data/series.yaml: every watched series with historical != "no" gets a fix_hook contract AND a matching {method, requires_fix: true, ages: all} entry. Both use {from}/{to}, exactly as listed in the failure report:
+   - amz-connect-summary (amazon guard)
+   - markable-spend (no amazon guard)
+   - meta-daily-metrics, http /api/cron/meta-sync?from={from}&to={to}
+   - pulse-daily-snapshots and pulse-weekly-stats, both http /api/cron/pulse-daily-sync?from={from}&to={to}
+   - pulse-signal-scores and pulse-market-health: from/to paths, NOT ?scope=full
+   - tma-cohort-showings (#tl-ext-repull=tma)
+   - recap-reports (#tl-ext-repull=recap)
+   - pulse-showings-weekly (ST hook, st_brief_sent guard)
+   Lost-data series (historical "no") keep no hook. Their gaps escalate as lost dates.
+2. lib/data.sh _fire: substitute {from}/{to} in http paths and check them against the ISO date regex, the same as for commands. In data_act, `to` = the END of the newest gap period: anchor+6 for weekly, the last day of the month for monthly, the day itself for daily.

+   - (d) P2: a count error at verify_at leaves failed_tries 0, the state REPULLING, and no new trigger. The next good count below the floor fails exactly one try.
+   - ADDITION (e): a requires_fix method NEVER fires while hook_merged is false. The series falls through to the operator or escalates as today, so none of the new methods can hit a live endpoint before its fix build merges.

+# Codex review - supervisor-v3-data
+
+## Cycle 1 (`codex review --base main`, HEAD 275c4b9, 2026-11-15 12:47 EST)
+
+Result: **1 P1, 1 P2, both REAL.** Per protocol the P1 stops the build: failure-report-supervisor-v3-data-codex.md proposes the fixes for operator triage. Cycle 2 has not run (cap 2).
+
+```
+codex
+Some advertised post-fix recovery hooks cannot be executed by the supervisor. Count outages also incorrectly consume verification failures and can initiate unnecessary remediation.
+
+Full review comments:
+- [P1] Register executable re-pull hooks for the remaining fix contracts — /Users/jimross/Projects/walkaway-cli/data/series.yaml:347-348
+  For a missing historical week in `pulse-weekly-stats`, the operator can build and merge the advertised hook, but `repull` remains empty, so `_method_for` still returns `none` after the merge. The supervisor never invokes the new hook and escalates instead of recovering the data. Several other entries likewise advertise `fix_hook` without a corresponding `requires_fix` method. Add executable methods for those contracts, including substitution of `{from}` and `{to}` in HTTP paths.
+- [P2] Require a successful fresh count before failing verification — /Users/jimross/Projects/walkaway-cli/lib/data.sh:660-663
+  If the count request fails when `verify_at` expires, `data_detect` leaves the gap REPULLING and returns successfully. This selection then treats the stale count as a confirmed failed re-pull, increments `failed_tries`, and can trigger another pull or an unnecessary fix build during a database outage. Defer verification for periods whose current count errored rather than consuming a try without observing their rows.
+```
+
+## Triage
+- P1 REAL: data/series.yaml advertises fix_hook on amz-connect-summary, markable-spend, meta-daily-metrics, pulse-daily-snapshots, pulse-weekly-stats, tma-cohort-showings, recap-reports with no requires_fix method, so after such a fix merges _method_for returns none and data-operator rule 2 escalates instead of re-pulling. _fire substitutes {from}/{to} only in commands, not http paths; weekly/monthly hooks would get the period anchors, not the period's full date range.
+- P2 REAL: data_act step 1 counts a failed try for any REPULLING gap past verify_at, even when this tick's count for that period errored (data_detect leaves the gap untouched on error), so a Supabase outage at verify time burns tries and can hand off to the operator.

+# Failure report - supervisor-v3-data - Codex cycle 1 (P1 stop)
+
+Phase 7 (Codex review, cycle 1 of 2) at HEAD 275c4b9 returned 1 P1 and 1 P2. Both are real
+(handoff/codex-review-supervisor-v3-data.md). Per WALKAWAY_BUILD_PROTOCOL.md a P1 is a stop rule, so the build stopped
+here. Nothing is broken live: data watching is ON and has been since 12:35 ET.
+- The only live gap so far is bb-showings-daily 11-09..11-13. It was ESCALATED 12:36 with one question (BrokerBay
+  session). The P1 cannot fire until a fix build merges.
+- The P2 needs a Supabase outage at the same moment as a verify.
+- Proposed fixes 1-2 are in scope: they make the plan's items 2 and 3 work as written. Neither touches a PROTECTED
+  action.
+
+## Where the build is
+- Phases 0-6 are committed (see build-log-supervisor-v3-data.md).
+  - Phase 6 smoke: AC1 final dump 21/21 PASS, inventory diff 0. AC3 live drill 18/18 (real claude; ntfy 8 polled ==
+    8 sent). Http re-pull path probed live (200 skipped). Phase 6 fixes R11/R12 shipped with fail-before/pass-after
+    evidence.
+  - AC4: data watching enabled 12:35:05 ET and supervisor reloaded 12:35:07. The first tick (12:35:16) checked all
+    21 series: 20 have no gaps; bb-showings-daily OPEN 11-09..11-13, then the operator ESCALATED it 12:36:09.
+- **Phase 6 is not finished.** These still need to be archived (handoff/phase-6-report-supervisor-v3-data.md lists
+  them):
+  - the BB 11-14 re-pull through the ST URL trigger: it becomes ready 13:00 ET, so the 13:00+ ticks fire it; evidence
+    is in data-repull.log, data/state/bb-showings-daily.json and supervisor.log;
+  - the final AC4/AC5 status archive.
+
+## P1 - executable hooks for every fix contract (REAL)
+`data/series.yaml` advertises a `fix_hook` on seven series with no `requires_fix` repull method: amz-connect-summary,
+markable-spend, meta-daily-metrics, pulse-daily-snapshots, pulse-weekly-stats, tma-cohort-showings, recap-reports.
+After such a fix merges, `_method_for` returns `none`, and data-operator rule 2 escalates instead of re-pulling.
+
+`_fire` substitutes `{from}`/`{to}` only in commands. Http paths are not substituted. Weekly and monthly gaps would
+pass period anchors, not the period's full date range.
+
+### Proposed fix
+1. **data/series.yaml**: every watched series whose source can re-pull (`historical` yes / partial / unknown) gets a
+   `fix_hook` contract AND a matching `{method, requires_fix: true, ages: all}` entry. Both use `{from}`/`{to}`.
+   - amz-connect-summary: `command open ... "https://audrey-ops-platform.vercel.app/#sr-ext-repull=connect_summary&from={from}&to={to}"`, guard amazon.
+   - markable-spend: `command open ... "#sr-ext-repull=markable_spend&from={from}&to={to}"` (Markable, not Amazon: no guard).
+   - meta-daily-metrics: `http /api/cron/meta-sync?from={from}&to={to}`.
+   - pulse-daily-snapshots: `http /api/cron/pulse-daily-sync?from={from}&to={to}`.
+   - pulse-weekly-stats: the same pulse-daily-sync from/to hook. Its contract says the range rolls up the weeks it
+     touches.
+   - pulse-signal-scores and pulse-market-health: add `fix_hook` + `http /api/cron/pulse-<x>?from={from}&to={to}`.
+     Their existing `?scope=full` recomputes ALL history with delete-by-geo (route.ts:43,56), so it is NOT used as a
+     re-pull.
+   - tma-cohort-showings: `command open ... "https://throughlinepro.com/#tl-ext-repull=tma&from={from}&to={to}"`.
+   - recap-reports: `command ... "#tl-ext-repull=recap&from={from}&to={to}"`.
+   - pulse-showings-weekly: the ST hook `#tl-ext-repull=st&from={from}&to={to}` with guard st_brief_sent. Re-ingesting
+     the week's days rolls it up.
+   - Lost-data series (`historical: "no"`: st-showings-forward, pulse-odds-snapshots, pulse-events-refresh,
+     curb-inquiry-results, pulse-member-snapshots) keep no hook. Their gaps escalate as lost dates.
+2. **lib/data.sh `_fire`**: substitute `{from}`/`{to}` in http paths, and check them against the ISO regex (as for
+   commands). In `data_act`, `to` = the END of the newest gap period: anchor + 6 days for weekly, the last day of
+   the month for monthly, the day itself for daily. So weekly and monthly hooks get full date ranges.
+3. **Regression tests** (each must FAIL on 275c4b9; archive the fail output and the pass output):
+   - (a) a series.yaml contract test: every watched series with historical != "no" has a fix_hook and a
+     `requires_fix` method whose command or path contains both `{from}` and `{to}`; every `requires_fix` method
+     matches its series' fix_hook trigger;
+   - (b) pulse-weekly-stats with a week gap, `hook_merged` true: the hook fires with
+     `from` = Monday and `to` = Sunday;
+   - (c) meta-daily-metrics with the stub trigger: the http path receives the substituted dates.
+
+## P2 - verify needs a successful fresh count (REAL)
+`data_act` step 1 counts a failed try for every REPULLING gap past `verify_at`, even when this tick's count for that
+period errored. `data_detect` leaves the gap untouched on an error. So a Supabase outage at verify time burns tries and
+can hand the gap to the operator.
+
+### Proposed fix
+- `data_detect` records `.gaps[p].counted_at = now` whenever that period was counted successfully.
+- `data_act` step 1 fails a try only if `counted_at >= verify_at`; otherwise the verify waits for the next good count.
+- Regression (must FAIL on 275c4b9): a REPULLING gap reaches verify_at while its fixture count errors. Expected:
+  `failed_tries` stays 0, the state stays REPULLING, and no new trigger fires. The next tick with a real count below
+  the floor fails the try exactly once.
+
+## Re-run after the fixes
+- `shellcheck -S warning` on all scripts (0).
+- Every tests/test-*.sh green (record the new counts).
+- `tests/drill/run-data-drill.sh` (must stay 18/18 on the drill topic).

+  - write the phase-6 report.

+# Phase 2 report - supervisor-v3-data (gap detector + gap states)
+
+- **Gap detector** (`lib/data.sh` data_detect / data_evaluate / data_tick):
+  - Covers every watched series over its lookback: 14 days, 4 weeks or 2 months.
+  - A period is judged only after `anchor + lag_days` at `ready_by`, so the series' own retry window is respected.
+  - A count below the floor is a gap: `missing` at 0 rows, `short` between 0 and the floor.
+  - Weekday cadence (`days:`) skips unscheduled weekdays entirely.
+- **Per-gap state** in `$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/<series>.json`:
+  - The plan's five states: OPEN / REPULLING / CLOSED / FIXING / ESCALATED.
+  - Each gap records rows_before, rows_now, rows_after, closed_via, failed_tries, tries_today and tries_day. Phase 2 drives OPEN and CLOSED; Phase 3 adds REPULLING; Phase 4 adds FIXING and ESCALATED.
+- **ntfy:** one line per series per tick for its newly OPEN gaps.
+  - Consecutive dates show as a range, e.g. `data bb-showings-daily: OPEN - 2026-11-09..11-14 missing (0/10 rows)`.
+  - CLOSED gets one line with rows before -> after.
+  - REPULLING is silent. Repeat ticks never re-notify.
+- **Cost:** complete periods are cached and re-counted once per local day (full scan, which catches rows deleted later). Other ticks count only the newly ready periods and the open gaps: one GET each.
+  - A tracked gap that ages out of the lookback is still counted (`_extra_periods`).
+- **Count errors:** never judged as gaps. 15 consecutive error ticks (`WALKAWAY_DATA_ERROR_TICKS`) send one ESCALATED line at priority high.
+- **CLI:** `data.sh tick | evaluate <s> | status | heartbeat | state <s>`.
+  - DATA table: SERIES, LAST COMPLETE, OPEN GAPS, LAST ACTION.
+  - Heartbeat: one line per series with open gaps.
+  - Not yet called by the supervisor; the hook lands in Phase 4, gated.
+- **tests/test-data-gaps.sh (new, 32/32):**
+  - D1 missing -> OPEN with one grouped ntfy; D2 short / exactly at the floor; D3 lag + ready_by; D4 weekday cadence.
+  - D5 CLOSED with before/after and one ntfy; D6 cache + daily full scan (14 -> 1 -> 14 counts); D7 ISO yearweek with lag 7; D8 errors escalate once.
+  - D9 runs the REAL series.yaml with real incident counts: odds 10-15..10-18 outage, cc 10-22 short 1/3, bb 11-09..11-14.
+- **Weekday support:** `days:` (jobs.yaml convention) was added. No real series needs it: Phase 0 found every pull runs daily, weekends included.

+# Phase 3 report - supervisor-v3-data (re-pull executor)
+
+- **data_act** (`lib/data.sh`) runs every tick after detection:
+  1. Verifies due tries: a REPULLING gap past verify_at whose rows did not reach the floor is a failed try (back to OPEN, silent).
+  2. Picks a method for each OPEN gap: the first repull entry covering the gap's age, the local hour (cc/spc run-now: D-2 before noon, D-1 after) and, for `requires_fix` hooks, a merged fix.
+  3. Fires each chosen method ONCE for all of its gaps (from = oldest, to = newest).
+  4. Hands the rest to the data operator (`need_operator` + reason).
+- **Trigger methods** (existing pull paths only):
+  - `run_now`: one PATCH of `extension_report_state.run_now_requested_at` for one report type from an allow-list (tracking_offsite, tracking_onsite, bounty, cc, spc, connect_summary), scoped to Audrey's user id. This is what the Showrunner run-now route writes. Any other type is refused before a request goes out.
+  - `http`: GET <app_url><path>. The Bearer CRON_SECRET is read at call time and goes to curl on stdin.
+  - `command`: the series' fixed command, with ISO {from}/{to}.
+  - `self_heal`: nothing fires; the wait is the try.
+  - Every trigger is logged to `$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/data-repull.log`, including the rows before.
+- **Guards:**
+  - Amazon: business hours 09:00-19:00 (Showrunner BUSINESS_HOURS); no CC batch in flight (read-only GET of today's cc_optin_runs + batches); one Amazon trigger per 15 min across all series.
+  - A deferral is not a try.
+  - Max 2 tries per gap per local day. Two failed tries (or a failed trigger rc) means `need_operator`; no third trigger.
+- **No path:** a gap whose age no method covers goes straight to the operator. The reason states when the source cannot re-pull a past period (historical "no").
+- **YAML 1.1 fix:** `historical: no` parsed as boolean false, so the values are now quoted strings. Caught by R7.
+- **tests/test-data-repull.sh (new, 28/28):**
+  - R1 grouped fire, silent REPULLING, CLOSED with before/after.
+  - R2 verify fails twice -> operator, no third try. R3 trigger rc counts as a try.
+  - R4 business hours / CC batch / pacing across two series. R5 the real cc hours window.
+  - R6 self-heal. R7 no path / lost data. R8 fix hook only after the merge. R9 the run-now allow-list.
+  - `tests/fixtures/data/trigger-stub.sh` can "land" rows.
+- test-data-gaps: two detection asserts now accept OPEN or REPULLING, because evaluate acts right after detect. Still 32/32.
+- Gates: shellcheck 0; 240/240 (data-gaps 32, data-repull 28, parse 7, states 18, v2-AC1 12, jobs-states 38, job-operator 37, operator 68).
+- No live trigger has fired: nothing calls data.sh from the supervisor yet, and every test uses the stub.

+# Phase 4 report - supervisor-v3-data (data operator, ntfy, heartbeat, supervisor hook, DATA table)
+
+- **lib/data-operator.sh (new).** Acts on gaps that data_act marks `need_operator`: no re-pull path for the gap's age, or a re-pull that failed twice. It reuses the job operator's read-only `claude -p` call, the repo-locked `_queue_or_launch` and the template-v2 conventions.
+  1. **Lost data:** the source cannot re-pull a past period (`historical: "no"`) and no path exists. ESCALATED with priority high, no model call. The question names the dates and rows, e.g. `scratch-lost lost 2026-11-15, 2026-11-16 (0/4 rows): the source cannot re-pull a past day. Accept the loss, or fix the pull so it stops missing days?`
+  2. **Post-fix failure:** a fix for the series merged and its hook re-pull failed twice. ESCALATED; no second fix loop.
+  3. **Fix already in flight** (registered and not merged, or still queued): the gaps join it as FIXING. No new plan.
+  4. **Otherwise:** one fix build per series per day. Read-only diagnosis, then either:
+     - an incident `job-incident-<series>-<date>.md` and a template-v2 plan whose `## Date-range re-pull hook` section quotes the series' `fix_hook` contract verbatim, plus the supervisor's exact call (AC "ACH"); the plan is queued or launched in the owner repo (FIXING);
+     - or ONE question (ESCALATED). The guards are the same as v2: protected, needs a live session, confidence below high, no what_to_build.
+  - `progress`: once the fix build is MERGED, the FIXING gaps go back to OPEN with `post_fix`, and `hook_merged` enables the series' `requires_fix` method. data_act then re-pulls exactly the gap dates and the count confirms CLOSED.
+- **templates/data-operator-prompt.md (new).** States that a re-pull is never protected. The fix to build is usually the date-range hook through the existing pull path; logins/sessions escalate.
+- **Supervisor hand-off (lib/data.sh):**
+  - `_data_spawn_operator` runs detached, once per set of gaps. If the operator exits without a decision on the same gaps, the next tick sends one ESCALATED; it never loops.
+  - `data_fix_progress` runs every tick while a fix is in flight.
+- **ntfy, one line per transition per series per tick:** OPEN, FIXING, CLOSED (rows before -> after, and via what), ESCALATED (priority high). REPULLING is silent.
+  - The 09/13/17 heartbeat lists only series with open gaps.
+  - lib/supervisor.sh gained a tick hook and a heartbeat hook, both gated on `$WALKAWAY_HOME/data.enabled` (absent live). The file was replaced atomically; the 12:00:24 live tick ran clean on the new file.
+- **`walkaway status` prints the DATA table** (SERIES, LAST COMPLETE, OPEN GAPS, LAST ACTION) after JOBS.
+- **Tests:**
+  - tests/test-data-operator.sh (new, 25/25):
+    - O1 lost dates -> ESCALATED, no model.
+    - O2 fix plan with every template-v2 section and the hook contract verbatim, launched with registry + parse-plan checks.
+    - O3 in flight. O4 merged -> hook fires once for exactly 11-08..11-09 -> CLOSED; OPEN 1 / FIXING 1 / CLOSED 1 / ESCALATED 0.
+    - O5 post-fix failure -> ESCALATED with no model or plan. O6 model escalation. O7 a silent operator -> exactly one ESCALATED.
+  - test-data-gaps D10 (supervisor gate, heartbeat, status table).
+- **Incident during this phase (contained):**
+  - test-data-gaps D9 uses the REAL series.yaml. Once the operator hook existed it spawned real read-only `claude -p` diagnoses (bb, cc) from a scratch WALKAWAY_HOME.
+  - They were killed within about a minute, before any decision.
+  - Verified: no fix-data branch or launch in either repo, real queue and registry untouched.
+  - The detection and re-pull suites now stub the operator (`tests/fixtures/data/operator-stub.sh`). Only test-data-operator runs the real operator, and it uses a stub claude.
+- Gates: shellcheck 0; 271/271 (data-gaps 38, data-operator 25, data-repull 28, parse 7, states 18, v2-AC1 12, jobs-states 38, job-operator 37, operator 68).

+# Data incident - bb-showings-daily - 2026-11-15
+
+Written by the walkaway data operator (read-only) at 2026-11-15 12:36 ET.
+
+## Gaps
+- 2026-11-09: missing - rows 0 / floor 10 (rows when opened: 0); no re-pull path for age 6; last try: none
+- 2026-11-10: missing - rows 0 / floor 10 (rows when opened: 0); no re-pull path for age 5; last try: none
+- 2026-11-11: missing - rows 0 / floor 10 (rows when opened: 0); no re-pull path for age 4; last try: none
+- 2026-11-12: missing - rows 0 / floor 10 (rows when opened: 0); no re-pull path for age 3; last try: none
+- 2026-11-13: missing - rows 0 / floor 10 (rows when opened: 0); no re-pull path for age 2; last try: none
+
+## Cause
+The BB code has not changed since the data stopped. The last commits to bb-core.js and manifest BB logic are 109318f5 (10-27) and 269dc378 (11-12), and 269dc378 only touched ST login and session reporting. BB days 11-01..11-08 landed 21/21 rows on that same code. The ST→BB sequence still fires every morning. /tmp/st-login-agent.log shows ST present every day 11-11..11-14, and phase-5-report-st-login-hardening.md:22 shows a URL-triggered run on 11-12 09:12 that reached "BB start". Even so, obs_date 11-09..11-13 have 0 rows. So runBbJob starts but writes nothing, and its failure is swallowed. In bb-core.js:908-915, a dead BB session sets bb:status 'bb_login' and returns {needsLogin:true}. maybeRunBbAfterSt (background.js:228-233) drops that return value, so no ntfy or retry ever happens. Zero rows or a Tableau error (bb-core.js:880-888, 913-914) only writes a bb_error status to chrome.storage. The actual BB status text exists only in the extension's storage on the Mini, which I can't read. The most likely cause is that the BrokerBay session logged out around 11-09/11-10, which looksLikeBbDeadSession then swallows. A Tableau viz change is also possible. A BB logout needs a human login, which is protected. Building the #tl-ext-repull=bb hook now would just re-run pullTableauDaily into the same failure, and the supervisor's post-merge verification would fail too. The hook is still the right fix once the session is confirmed alive. The silent needsLogin swallow should also get a phone ping in that same plan.
+
+## Evidence
+- check: obs_date 2026-11-08..11-01 count=21 each (ok); 2026-11-09, 11-10, 11-11, 11-12, 11-13 count=0 (missing); repull_log=[]
+- /tmp/st-login-agent.log: 2026-11-13 07:05:04 decision=SILENT_PRESENT yesterday=2026-11-12 present=1 ext_last_outcome_today=login_ok; 2026-11-14 07:05:05 ... yesterday=2026-11-13 present=1; 2026-11-15 07:05:02 ... yesterday=2026-11-14 present=1 (ST pull runs daily, so the BB trigger fires daily)
+- handoff/phase-5-report-st-login-hardening.md:20-23: URL trigger 11-12 09:10:31 -> 'Daily pull complete: 8 ok' 09:12:15 -> BB start; yet BB obs_date 2026-11-11 has 0 rows
+- git log -- tools/showings-daily-pull/bb-core.js: last BB logic change 109318f5 2026-10-27; 269dc378 (11-12) touched ST login/session reporting only (background.js, pull-core.js, offscreen.js, popup)
+- tools/showings-daily-pull/bb-core.js:908-911: dead BB session -> setBbStatus bb_login + return {needsLogin:true}, no notify

+# Phase 0 inventory - supervisor-v3-data (2026-11-15, read-only)
+
+Every scheduled pull / cron / launchd agent in both owner repos, discovered from code (file:line below) and
+confirmed against live Supabase rows (REST GETs with the service-role key read from each repo's `.env.local` at
+call time; never printed). No writes, no triggers. The machine-readable id list is `phase0-inventory.txt`;
+`data/series.yaml` must name every id (watched or excluded with a reason): that diff is AC1.
+
+Repos: Showrunner = `~/Projects/audrey-ops-platform` (source `audrey`, user `d3a83609-...`), Throughline =
+`~/Projects/Throughline/throughline-app` (source `throughline`, pulse team `d14dce4f-...`).
+
+## Floors (formula, applied to every watched series)
+30-day (daily) / 8-week (weekly) per-period row counts over the periods present (`phase0-floors.tsv`):
+- every period has the same count c -> floor = c ("structural": e.g. one ST total row per day, 2 connect programs);
+- otherwise floor = max(1, floor(median / 2)) (the plan's "rows >= 50% of 30-day median").
+Monthly `pulse-member-snapshots`: 10082 / 10131 / 10161 rows on 09-01 / 10-01 / 11-01 -> floor 5065 (Phase 1 corrected an arithmetic slip, 5040).
+`pulse-odds-snapshots` uses the 50% rule (22), not structural 43: one row per market, and 2026-10-05..10-11 had 3 rows/day
+(found by the Phase 1 as-of check), so the count moves as markets roll off.
+

+## Re-pull guards (plan item 2)
+- Amazon (all `amz-*` triggers): business hours 09:00-19:00 ET = Showrunner's own `BUSINESS_HOURS`
+  (`extension/cc-optin-lib.js:53`); never while a CC batch is in flight = today's `cc_optin_runs` status PLANNED or
+  RUNNING, or any `cc_optin_batches` row SUBMITTED (`20261024000001_cc_optin.sql:9-11`); paced: one Amazon trigger
+  per 15 minutes across all series.
+- Max 2 tries per gap per day; a try counts only when a trigger fired (a guard deferral is not a try).
+- A RE-PULL of data is never protected (Jim 11/15). The run-now trigger writes only
+  `extension_report_state.run_now_requested_at` for one base report type (what the Showrunner run-now route itself
+  sets); the HTTP triggers are the routes' own cron GETs; the ST trigger is the URL the ST login agent opens.
