+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# AC3 live drill for data-series gap watching (supervisor-v3-data). Real supervisor ticks (lib/supervisor.sh tick with
+# data watching on) in a scratch $WALKAWAY_HOME, time travel via WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW, REAL headless `claude -p`
+# diagnosis, REAL ntfy pushes to a drill topic (captured via the ntfy JSON poll API). Delete-free: the series are
+# scratch series whose row counts live in fixture files; the "pulls" are scratch scripts that write those fixtures.
+# Nothing reads or writes a real table, and no real pull is triggered.
+#
+# The owner repo is a throwaway scratch repo (origin = a scratch bare repo) and a registered busy build holds its
+# one-builder slot, so the fix plan is QUEUED and never launched.
+#
+#   series         gap                                re-pull path                       expected ntfy
+#   drill-repull   2026-11-17 missing (age 1)         land.sh (an existing path, works)  OPEN, CLOSED
+#   drill-nopath   2026-11-12 missing (age 6)         scripts/pull.sh (yesterday only)   OPEN, FIXING, CLOSED
+#                                                     -> 2 failed tries -> claude -> fix plan (--from/--to hook)
+#                                                     queued -> simulated merge -> hook re-pulls 11-12 -> CLOSED
+#   drill-lost     2026-11-16 missing (age 2)         none; historical "no"              OPEN, ESCALATED
+# Evidence: handoff/supervisor-v3-data-evidence/ac3-drill/.
+#
+#   tests/drill/run-data-drill.sh            run the drill
+#   tests/drill/run-data-drill.sh --clean    remove the scratch world
+
+set -uo pipefail
+
+HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
+ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
+WORK="$HERE/work-data"
+EVID="$ROOT/handoff/supervisor-v3-data-evidence/ac3-drill"
+TOPIC="${DRILL_TOPIC:-jim-walkaway-data-drill-$(openssl rand -hex 4)}"
+
+if [[ "${1:-}" == "--clean" ]]; then
+  for f in "$WORK"/home/registry/*.json; do [[ -f "$f" ]] && kill "$(jq -r .builder_pid "$f")" 2>/dev/null; done
+  rm -rf "$WORK"; echo "data drill torn down"; exit 0
+fi
+
+rm -rf "$WORK" "$EVID"
+mkdir -p "$WORK/fx" "$WORK/logs" "$WORK/bin" "$EVID"
+T0=$(date +%s)
+TRANSCRIPT="$EVID/drill-transcript.txt"
+say() { echo "[$(date '+%H:%M:%S')] $*" | tee -a "$TRANSCRIPT"; }
+PASS=0
+FAIL=0
+check() { local label="$1"; shift; if "$@"; then say "PASS: $label"; PASS=$((PASS + 1)); else say "FAIL: $label"; FAIL=$((FAIL + 1)); fi; }
+
+export WALKAWAY_HOME="$WORK/home" NTFY_TOPIC="$TOPIC" WALKAWAY_HEARTBEAT_HOURS=off WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="$WORK/series.yaml"
+export WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES="$WORK/fx" WALKAWAY_GH_CMD="$HERE/gh-sim.sh" GH_SIM_DIR="$WORK/gh"
+export WALKAWAY_CLAUDE_PROJECTS="$WORK/none" WALKAWAY_LAUNCH_LOG_DIR="$WORK/logs" WALKAWAY_STALL_SECS=86400
+unset WALKAWAY_NTFY_DISABLED WALKAWAY_OPERATOR_CMD WALKAWAY_DATA_OPERATOR WALKAWAY_DATA_TRIGGER_CMD WALKAWAY_DATA_GUARD_FIXTURE
+mkdir -p "$WALKAWAY_HOME"
+touch "$WALKAWAY_HOME/supervisor.enabled" "$WALKAWAY_HOME/data.enabled"
+say "data drill start: topic $TOPIC, home $WALKAWAY_HOME"
+
+# ── fixture "store" + the scratch pull scripts ───────────────────────────────
+# land.sh <series> <from> <to> <count>: write <count> rows for every day from..to (stands in for a working pull).
+cat > "$WORK/bin/land.sh" <<'SH'
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+python3 - "$WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES/$1.json" "$2" "$3" "$4" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, datetime, os
+p, a, b, c = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], int(sys.argv[4])
+d = json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else {}
+x = datetime.date.fromisoformat(a); e = datetime.date.fromisoformat(b)
+while x <= e:
+    d[x.isoformat()] = c; x += datetime.timedelta(days=1)
+json.dump(d, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+SH
+chmod +x "$WORK/bin/land.sh"
+fill() { "$WORK/bin/land.sh" "$@"; }
+
+# Scratch owner repo: a daily pull that can only fetch YESTERDAY (the drill's "no date-range path" cause).
+REPO="$WORK/pull-drill"
+git init -q --bare "$WORK/pull-drill-origin.git"
+git init -q -b main "$REPO"
+mkdir -p "$REPO/scripts" "$REPO/handoff"
+cat > "$REPO/scripts/pull.sh" <<'SH'
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Daily showings pull (drill throwaway). Scheduled 06:00 ET. Fetches YESTERDAY's rows from the source API and
+# upserts them into daily_showings (drill: the store is $WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES/drill-nopath.json).
+# Usage: scripts/pull.sh            no arguments: the date is always yesterday (see fetch_day below)
+set -euo pipefail
+
+fetch_day() {   # $1 = YYYY-MM-DD; the source API accepts any past date (GET /showings?date=...)
+  python3 - "$WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES/drill-nopath.json" "$1" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, os
+p, d = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
+s = json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else {}
+s[d] = 12
+json.dump(s, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+}
+
+day=$(date -v-1d +%F)   # always yesterday: there is no way to ask for another date
+fetch_day "$day"
+echo "pulled $day"
+SH
+chmod +x "$REPO/scripts/pull.sh"
+printf '# pull-drill (throwaway)\nscripts/pull.sh: the daily pull. Table daily_showings, one row-set per day (floor 10).\n' > "$REPO/README.md"
+git -C "$REPO" add -A
+git -C "$REPO" -c user.name=drill -c user.email=drill@example.invalid commit -qm "daily pull"
+git -C "$REPO" remote add origin "$WORK/pull-drill-origin.git"
+git -C "$REPO" push -q -u origin main 2>/dev/null
+git -C "$REPO" remote set-head origin main
+
+sleep 3600 > /dev/null 2>&1 &
+BUSY=$!
+bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/registry.sh'; registry_register drill-busy '$REPO' '$REPO' '$REPO/handoff/build-plan-drill-busy.md' main '$BUSY' '' '' false" > /dev/null
+say "drill-busy registered in $REPO (pid $BUSY): holds the one-builder slot so the fix plan queues"
+
+# ── series config (JSON is valid YAML) ───────────────────────────────────────
+jq -n --arg repo "$REPO" --arg land "$WORK/bin/land.sh" '{
+  defaults: {lookback_days: 14, lookback_weeks: 4, lookback_months: 2, max_tries_per_day: 2, verify_after_mins: 30},
+  vars: {}, sources: {scratch: {env_file: "/dev/null", url_var: "X", key_var: "Y", app_url: "http://127.0.0.1:9", cron_secret_var: "Z"}},
+  guards: {},
+  series: [
+    {name: "drill-repull", title: "Drill: a series whose existing re-pull works", watch: true, owner_repo: $repo, source: "scratch",
+     table: "t_repull", key: {column: "d", type: "date"}, filters: "", cadence: "daily", lag_days: 1, ready_by: "12:00",
+     floor: 10, floor_basis: "drill", historical: "yes",
+     repull: [{method: "command", ages: [1, 2, 3], verify_after_mins: 30, command: ($land + " drill-repull {from} {to} 12")}]},
+    {name: "drill-nopath", title: "Drill: daily showings pull (scripts/pull.sh fetches yesterday only)", watch: true, owner_repo: $repo, source: "scratch",
+     table: "daily_showings", key: {column: "obs_date", type: "date"}, filters: "", cadence: "daily", lag_days: 1, ready_by: "12:00",
+     floor: 10, floor_basis: "drill", historical: "yes",
+     repull: [{method: "command", ages: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8], verify_after_mins: 30, command: ("bash " + $repo + "/scripts/pull.sh"), note: "re-runs the scheduled pull: it fetches yesterday only"},
+              {method: "command", requires_fix: true, ages: "all", verify_after_mins: 30, command: ("bash " + $repo + "/scripts/pull.sh --from {from} --to {to}")}],
+     fix_hook: "scripts/pull.sh --from YYYY-MM-DD --to YYYY-MM-DD pulls exactly that inclusive date range through fetch_day (the existing fetch/upsert path); no arguments keeps today'"'"'s behaviour (yesterday)"},
+    {name: "drill-lost", title: "Drill: an as-of snapshot (cannot re-pull a past day)", watch: true, owner_repo: $repo, source: "scratch",
+     table: "t_snapshot", key: {column: "d", type: "date"}, filters: "", cadence: "daily", lag_days: 0, ready_by: "13:00",
+     floor: 4, floor_basis: "drill", historical: "no",
+     repull: [{method: "command", ages: [0], verify_after_mins: 30, command: ($land + " drill-lost {from} {to} 4")}]}
+  ]}' > "$WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE"
+
+# Delete-free simulation: 14 days of rows, then holes. Nothing is deleted anywhere: the holes are fixture counts.
+fill drill-repull 2026-11-01 2026-11-16 12
+fill drill-nopath 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; fill drill-nopath 2026-11-12 2026-11-12 0
+fill drill-lost 2026-11-01 2026-11-18 4; fill drill-lost 2026-11-16 2026-11-16 0
+
+at() { TZ=America/New_York date -j -f '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M' "2026-11-18 $1" +%s; }
+tick() { say "supervisor tick @ 2026-11-18 $1 ET (time travel)"; WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW=$(at "$1") bash "$ROOT/lib/supervisor.sh" tick >> "$WORK/supervisor.log" 2>&1; }
+dst() { jq -r --arg p "$2" ".gaps[\$p].$3 // empty" "$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/$1.json" 2>/dev/null; }
+sget() { jq -r ".$2 // empty" "$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/$1.json" 2>/dev/null; }
+wait_operator() { # series: wait (<= 30 min) for the data operator to finish
+  local i p
+  for i in $(seq 1 360); do
+    p=$(sget "$1" operator_pid)
+    { [[ -z "$p" ]] || ! kill -0 "$p" 2>/dev/null; } && { say "$1 operator done after $((i * 5))s"; return 0; }
+    sleep 5
+  done
+  say "$1 operator still running after 30 min"
+}
+
+# ── 1: 12:30 gaps open; re-pulls fire ────────────────────────────────────────
+tick 12:30
+check "drill-repull 11-17 OPEN -> re-pull fired (REPULLING)" [ "$(dst drill-repull 2026-11-17 state)" == "REPULLING" ]
+check "drill-nopath 11-12 OPEN -> scheduled pull re-run (REPULLING)" [ "$(dst drill-nopath 2026-11-12 state)" == "REPULLING" ]
+wait_operator drill-lost
+check "drill-lost 11-16: source cannot re-pull -> ESCALATED (lost)" bash -c "[[ '$(dst drill-lost 2026-11-16 state)' == ESCALATED && '$(dst drill-lost 2026-11-16 lost)' == true ]]"
+
+# ── 2: 12:32 the working re-pull landed rows -> CLOSED ───────────────────────
+tick 12:32
+check "drill-repull CLOSED rows 0 -> 12" bash -c "[[ '$(dst drill-repull 2026-11-17 state)' == CLOSED && '$(dst drill-repull 2026-11-17 rows_after)' == 12 ]]"
+check "drill-nopath still REPULLING inside its verify window" [ "$(dst drill-nopath 2026-11-12 state)" == "REPULLING" ]
+
+# ── 3: the yesterday-only pull never lands 11-12: two failed tries -> data operator (real claude) ──
+tick 13:01
+check "drill-nopath verify failed -> try 2 fired" bash -c "[[ '$(dst drill-nopath 2026-11-12 failed_tries)' == 1 && '$(dst drill-nopath 2026-11-12 state)' == REPULLING ]]"
+tick 13:32
+wait_operator drill-nopath
+INC="$WALKAWAY_HOME/data-fixes/drill-nopath-2026-11-18/handoff/job-incident-drill-nopath-2026-11-18.md"
+PLAN=$(ls "$WALKAWAY_HOME"/data-fixes/drill-nopath-2026-11-18/handoff/build-plan-fix-data-drill-nopath-*.md 2>/dev/null | head -1)
+say "drill-nopath decision: $(sed -n '/^## Decision/{n;p;}' "$INC" 2>/dev/null)"
+check "incident written (gaps, cause, evidence, fresh counts)" bash -c "grep -q '2026-11-12: missing - rows 0 / floor 10' '$INC' && grep -q '^## Cause' '$INC' && grep -q '^## Evidence' '$INC' && grep -q 'Fresh read-only counts' '$INC'"
+check "drill-nopath FIXING: template-v2 fix plan queued behind drill-busy" bash -c "[[ '$(dst drill-nopath 2026-11-12 state)' == FIXING && -n '$PLAN' ]] && grep -qxF '$PLAN' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/queue/pull-drill.txt'"
+check "fix plan not launched (no registry entry)" bash -c "! ls '$WALKAWAY_HOME'/registry/fix-data-drill-nopath-*.json > /dev/null 2>&1"
+check "plan: template-v2 sections, owner repo, hook contract verbatim" bash -c "for s in '## Feature name' '## Branch name' '## Repo' '## Goal' '## Evidence' '## Quality rules' '## Date-range re-pull hook' '## Source of truth' '## Acceptance criteria' '## Gate checklist' '## Escalate to Jim' '## Phases' '## Do NOT build' '## Stop rules'; do grep -q \"^\$s\" '$PLAN' || exit 1; done; grep -qF '$REPO' '$PLAN' && grep -qF 'scripts/pull.sh --from YYYY-MM-DD --to YYYY-MM-DD pulls exactly that inclusive date range' '$PLAN'"
+
+# ── 4: the fix "merges": the hook exists; the supervisor re-pulls exactly 11-12 -> CLOSED ──
+FIXF=$(basename "$PLAN" .md); FIXF=${FIXF#build-plan-}
+cat > "$REPO/scripts/pull.sh" <<'SH'
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Daily showings pull (drill throwaway) - after the simulated fix: --from/--to re-pulls a date range.
+set -euo pipefail
+fetch_day() {
+  python3 - "$WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES/drill-nopath.json" "$1" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, os
+p, d = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
+s = json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else {}
+s[d] = 12
+json.dump(s, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+}
+if [[ "${1:-}" == "--from" ]]; then
+  d="$2"; while [[ ! "$d" > "$4" ]]; do fetch_day "$d"; echo "pulled $d"; d=$(date -j -v+1d -f %F "$d" +%F); done
+else
+  fetch_day "$(date -v-1d +%F)"
+fi
+SH
+git -C "$REPO" -c user.name=drill -c user.email=drill@example.invalid commit -qam "simulated $FIXF: --from/--to hook"
+bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/registry.sh'; registry_register '$FIXF' '$REPO' '$REPO' '$PLAN' 'feature/$FIXF' '' '' '' false; registry_update '$FIXF' '.state = \"MERGED\" | .last_action = \"drill: simulated merge\"'" > /dev/null
+say "simulated merge of $FIXF (registry MERGED; scratch repo now has the --from/--to hook)"
+tick 14:00
+check "post-merge: the hook fired once for exactly 2026-11-12..2026-11-12" bash -c "grep -q 'drill-nopath: command bash .*/scripts/pull.sh --from 2026-11-12 --to 2026-11-12' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/data-repull.log' && [[ \$(grep -c 'pull.sh --from' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/data-repull.log') -eq 1 ]]"
+tick 14:02
+tick 14:04
+check "drill-nopath CLOSED rows 0 -> 12 via the fix hook" bash -c "[[ '$(dst drill-nopath 2026-11-12 state)' == CLOSED && '$(dst drill-nopath 2026-11-12 rows_after)' == 12 ]] && [[ '$(dst drill-nopath 2026-11-12 closed_via)' == 'command (fix hook): '* ]]"
+
+# ── capture + verify ntfy ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+sleep 5
+curl -s "https://ntfy.sh/$TOPIC/json?poll=1&since=$T0" > "$EVID/ntfy-poll.json"
+cp "$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log" "$EVID/ntfy-local.log"
+jq -r 'select(.event == "message") | [(.time | strftime("%H:%M:%S")), (.priority // 3 | tostring), .message] | @tsv' "$EVID/ntfy-poll.json" > "$EVID/ntfy-poll-messages.tsv"
+say "ntfy poll: $(wc -l < "$EVID/ntfy-poll-messages.tsv" | tr -d ' ') messages on $TOPIC; local sends: $(wc -l < "$EVID/ntfy-local.log" | tr -d ' ')"
+check "ntfy poll == local send log (1:1)" bash -c "[[ \"\$(cut -f3- '$EVID/ntfy-poll-messages.tsv' | sort)\" == \"\$(cut -f4- '$EVID/ntfy-local.log' | sort)\" ]]"
+seq_of() { cut -f3 "$EVID/ntfy-poll-messages.tsv" | grep "^data $1: " | sed -E 's/^data [a-z-]+: ([A-Z]+) - .*/\1/' | tr '\n' ' '; }
+say "polled transitions: drill-repull [$(seq_of drill-repull)] drill-nopath [$(seq_of drill-nopath)] drill-lost [$(seq_of drill-lost)]"
+check "drill-repull: OPEN, CLOSED - one ntfy each" [ "$(seq_of drill-repull)" == "OPEN CLOSED " ]
+check "drill-nopath: OPEN, FIXING, CLOSED - one ntfy each" [ "$(seq_of drill-nopath)" == "OPEN FIXING CLOSED " ]
+check "drill-lost: OPEN, ESCALATED - one ntfy each" [ "$(seq_of drill-lost)" == "OPEN ESCALATED " ]
+check "ESCALATED is priority high (ntfy 4) on the wire" bash -c "awk -F'\t' '\$3 ~ /^data drill-lost: ESCALATED/ {print \$2}' '$EVID/ntfy-poll-messages.tsv' | grep -qx 4"
+check "REPULLING never pinged" bash -c "! grep -q 'REPULLING' '$EVID/ntfy-poll-messages.tsv'"
+
+# ── archive ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+cp "$INC" "$EVID/" 2>/dev/null
+[[ -n "$PLAN" ]] && cp "$PLAN" "$EVID/"
+cp "$WALKAWAY_HOME"/data-fixes/drill-lost-2026-11-18/handoff/job-incident-*.md "$EVID/" 2>/dev/null
+cp "$WALKAWAY_HOME"/data-fixes/drill-nopath-2026-11-18/handoff/evidence-*.json "$EVID/" 2>/dev/null
+mkdir -p "$EVID/state"; cp "$WALKAWAY_HOME"/data/state/drill-*.json "$EVID/state/"
+cp "$WALKAWAY_HOME"/queue/*.txt "$EVID/" 2>/dev/null
+cp "$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/data-repull.log" "$EVID/data-repull.log" 2>/dev/null
+grep -h 'data:' "$WORK/supervisor.log" > "$EVID/supervisor-data-transitions.log" 2>/dev/null
+cat "$WALKAWAY_HOME"/logs/data-operator-*.log > "$EVID/data-operator.log" 2>/dev/null
+cp "$WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE" "$EVID/drill-series.json"
+WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW=$(at 14:04) bash "$ROOT/walkaway.sh" status > "$EVID/walkaway-status-drill.txt" 2>&1
+kill "$BUSY" 2>/dev/null
+
+say "data drill done: $PASS passed, $FAIL failed (topic $TOPIC)"
+[[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ]]

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Re-pull trigger stub (WALKAWAY_DATA_TRIGGER_CMD) for tests and the AC3 drill.
+#   trigger-stub.sh <series> <method> <arg> <from> <to>
+# Appends one line to $WALKAWAY_HOME/triggers.log (and the resolved call to triggers-resolved.log). STUB_RC = exit
+# code (default 0). STUB_LAND=<count> "lands" that many rows for every day from..to in the fixture counts
+# ($WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES/<series>.json), like a re-pull whose rows arrive before the verify tick.
+set -uo pipefail
+echo "$1 $2 $3 $4 $5" >> "${WALKAWAY_HOME}/triggers.log"
+# The command / http path the supervisor would really call (dates substituted), when lib/data.sh passes it.
+[[ -n "${WALKAWAY_DATA_TRIGGER_RESOLVED+x}" ]] && echo "$1 $2 $WALKAWAY_DATA_TRIGGER_RESOLVED" >> "${WALKAWAY_HOME}/triggers-resolved.log"
+if [[ -n "${STUB_LAND:-}" && -n "${WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES:-}" ]]; then
+  python3 - "${WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES}/$1.json" "$4" "$5" "$STUB_LAND" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, datetime, os
+p, a, b, c = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], int(sys.argv[4])
+d = json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else {}
+x = datetime.date.fromisoformat(a); e = datetime.date.fromisoformat(b)
+while x <= e:
+    d[x.isoformat()] = c; x += datetime.timedelta(days=1)
+json.dump(d, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+fi
+exit "${STUB_RC:-0}"

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Gap detection + gap state tests (lib/data.sh) on scratch series (tests/fixtures/data/series-test.yaml) and on the
+# REAL data/series.yaml entries with fixture counts from real incidents. No network: counts come from
+# WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES, time is WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW, ntfy is disabled (lines are still logged).
+# shellcheck disable=SC2034  # n1/n2 are read inside check's eval strings
+set -uo pipefail
+
+ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
+PASS=0; FAIL=0
+ok()   { PASS=$((PASS + 1)); echo "  ok   $1"; }
+bad()  { FAIL=$((FAIL + 1)); echo "  FAIL $1"; }
+check() { if eval "$2"; then ok "$1"; else bad "$1"; fi; }
+
+export WALKAWAY_NTFY_DISABLED=1 WALKAWAY_HEARTBEAT_HOURS=off
+TEST_YAML="$ROOT/tests/fixtures/data/series-test.yaml"
+
+fresh() { # fresh scratch home + fixture dir
+  S=$(mktemp -d); mkdir -p "$S/fx"
+  export WALKAWAY_HOME="$S" WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES="$S/fx" WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="$TEST_YAML" WALKAWAY_DATA_COUNT_LOG="$S/counts.log"
+  export WALKAWAY_DATA_TRIGGER_CMD="$ROOT/tests/fixtures/data/trigger-stub.sh" WALKAWAY_DATA_OPERATOR="$ROOT/tests/fixtures/data/operator-stub.sh"
+}
+# opened <series> <period>: the gap was opened (data_evaluate may already have moved it on to REPULLING)
+opened() { [[ "$(gst "$1" "$2" state)" == OPEN || "$(gst "$1" "$2" state)" == REPULLING ]]; }
+at() { export WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW; WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW=$(TZ=America/New_York date -j -f '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M' "$1" +%s); }
+# fill <series> <from> <to> <count>: set fixture counts for every day in [from, to]
+fill() {
+  python3 - "$S/fx/$1.json" "$2" "$3" "$4" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, datetime, os
+p, a, b, c = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], int(sys.argv[4])
+d = json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else {}
+x = datetime.date.fromisoformat(a); e = datetime.date.fromisoformat(b)
+while x <= e:
+    d[x.isoformat()] = c; x += datetime.timedelta(days=1)
+json.dump(d, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+}
+setc() { python3 - "$S/fx/$1.json" "$2" "$3" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, os
+p = sys.argv[1]; d = json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else {}
+d[sys.argv[2]] = int(sys.argv[3]); json.dump(d, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+}
+ev() { bash "$ROOT/lib/data.sh" evaluate "$1" > /dev/null 2>&1; }
+gst() { jq -r --arg p "$2" ".gaps[\$p].$3 // empty" "$S/data/state/$1.json" 2>/dev/null; }
+ntfy_n() { local n; n=$(grep -c "data $1: $2" "$S/logs/ntfy.log" 2>/dev/null); echo "${n:-0}"; }
+
+echo "D1 missing days past ready_by -> OPEN, one grouped ntfy listing the dates"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; setc scratch-daily 2026-11-12 0; setc scratch-daily 2026-11-13 0; setc scratch-daily 2026-11-14 0
+ev scratch-daily
+check "11-12 OPEN missing" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-12 state)" == OPEN && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-12 status)" == missing ]]'
+check "11-14 OPEN, rows_before 0" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-14 state)" == OPEN && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-14 rows_before)" == 0 ]]'
+check "complete days are not gaps" '[[ -z "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-15 state)" ]]'
+check "exactly one OPEN ntfy, consecutive dates as a range" '[[ "$(ntfy_n scratch-daily OPEN)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "OPEN - 2026-11-12..11-14 missing (0/10 rows)" "$S/logs/ntfy.log"'
+ev scratch-daily
+check "a second tick does not re-notify" '[[ "$(ntfy_n scratch-daily OPEN)" == 1 ]]'
+
+echo "D2 short day (under the floor) -> OPEN short with rows"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; setc scratch-daily 2026-11-16 4
+ev scratch-daily
+check "11-16 opened short rows 4" 'opened scratch-daily 2026-11-16 && [[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 status)" == short && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 rows_before)" == 4 ]]'
+check "ntfy says short 4/10" 'grep -q "OPEN - 2026-11-16 short (4/10 rows)" "$S/logs/ntfy.log"'
+setc scratch-daily 2026-11-16 10
+ev scratch-daily
+check "exactly at the floor = complete -> CLOSED" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 state)" == CLOSED ]]'
+
+echo "D3 lag-aware: the newest period is not judged before lag + ready_by"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 11:59"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-16 12      # 11-17 has no rows yet (lands 11-18 by 12:00)
+ev scratch-daily
+check "11-17 not a gap at 11:59 on 11-18" '[[ -z "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-17 state)" ]]'
+check "today (lag 1) never judged" '[[ -z "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-18 state)" ]]'
+at "2026-11-18 12:00"; ev scratch-daily
+check "11-17 opened at its ready_by 12:00" 'opened scratch-daily 2026-11-17'
+
+echo "D4 weekday cadence: weekend days without rows are not gaps"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-weekday 2026-11-02 2026-11-17 3; setc scratch-weekday 2026-11-14 0; setc scratch-weekday 2026-11-15 0; setc scratch-weekday 2026-11-16 0
+ev scratch-weekday
+check "Sat 11-14 / Sun 11-15 not gaps" '[[ -z "$(gst scratch-weekday 2026-11-14 state)" && -z "$(gst scratch-weekday 2026-11-15 state)" ]]'
+check "Mon 11-16 missing -> OPEN" '[[ "$(gst scratch-weekday 2026-11-16 state)" == OPEN ]]'
+check "weekend periods never counted" '! grep -qE "scratch-weekday 2026-11-1[45]" "$S/counts.log"'
+
+echo "D5 rows land -> CLOSED with rows before/after, one CLOSED ntfy"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; setc scratch-daily 2026-11-10 0; setc scratch-daily 2026-11-11 3
+ev scratch-daily
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-10 2026-11-11 11
+at "2026-11-18 13:02"; ev scratch-daily
+check "both CLOSED" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-10 state)" == CLOSED && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-11 state)" == CLOSED ]]'
+check "rows before/after recorded" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-11 rows_before)" == 3 && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-11 rows_after)" == 11 ]]'
+check "one CLOSED ntfy with before -> after" '[[ "$(ntfy_n scratch-daily CLOSED)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "CLOSED - 2026-11-10 rows 0 -> 11, 2026-11-11 rows 3 -> 11" "$S/logs/ntfy.log"'
+at "2026-11-18 13:04"; ev scratch-daily
+check "no repeat CLOSED ntfy" '[[ "$(ntfy_n scratch-daily CLOSED)" == 1 ]]'
+check "last_complete = 11-17" '[[ "$(jq -r .last_complete "$S/data/state/scratch-daily.json")" == 2026-11-17 ]]'
+
+echo "D6 cache: complete periods are counted once per day (full scan), gaps every tick"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; setc scratch-daily 2026-11-12 0
+ev scratch-daily; n1=$(wc -l < "$S/counts.log")
+at "2026-11-18 13:02"; ev scratch-daily; n2=$(( $(wc -l < "$S/counts.log") - n1 ))
+check "first tick counts all 14 ready periods" '[[ "$n1" -eq 14 ]]'
+check "second tick counts only the open gap" '[[ "$n2" -eq 1 ]] && tail -1 "$S/counts.log" | grep -q "scratch-daily 2026-11-12"'
+at "2026-11-19 13:00"; ev scratch-daily
+check "next day: full re-scan (catches rows deleted after the fact)" '[[ "$(( $(wc -l < "$S/counts.log") - n1 - n2 ))" -ge 14 ]]'
+
+echo "D7 weekly (ISO yearweek key, lag 7)"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"   # Wed of week 47; week 46 (anchor 11-09) is ready since Mon 11-16 07:00
+for a in 2026-10-19 2026-10-26 2026-11-02 2026-11-09 2026-11-16; do setc scratch-weekly "$a" 500; done
+setc scratch-weekly 2026-11-02 40
+ev scratch-weekly
+check "week of 11-02 short (40 < 100) -> OPEN" '[[ "$(gst scratch-weekly 2026-11-02 state)" == OPEN ]]'
+check "current week 11-16 not judged (lag 7)" '[[ -z "$(gst scratch-weekly 2026-11-16 state)" ]] && ! grep -q "scratch-weekly 2026-11-16" "$S/counts.log"'
+check "yearweek key is ISO %G%V" '[[ "$(WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW=$WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW bash "$ROOT/lib/data.sh" check scratch-weekly | jq -r ".periods[] | select(.period == \"2026-11-02\") | .key")" == 202645 ]]'
+
+echo "D8 count errors: never judged as gaps; escalate once after N ticks"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+echo '{"__error":"HTTP 503"}' > "$S/fx/scratch-daily.json"
+export WALKAWAY_DATA_ERROR_TICKS=3
+ev scratch-daily; ev scratch-daily
+check "no gaps from errors" '[[ "$(jq -r ".gaps | length" "$S/data/state/scratch-daily.json")" == 0 ]]'
+check "no ESCALATED before N" '[[ "$(ntfy_n scratch-daily ESCALATED)" == 0 ]]'
+ev scratch-daily; ev scratch-daily
+check "exactly one ESCALATED (priority high) at N" '[[ "$(ntfy_n scratch-daily ESCALATED)" == 1 ]] && grep "data scratch-daily: ESCALATED" "$S/logs/ntfy.log" | grep -q "	high	"'
+unset WALKAWAY_DATA_ERROR_TICKS
+
+echo "D9 REAL series.yaml + real incident counts (fixtures)"
+fresh; export WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="$ROOT/data/series.yaml"
+# pulse_odds_snapshots 2026-10-15..10-18: 0 rows (real Throughline cron outage); 43 rows on the days around it
+at "2026-10-19 09:00"; fill pulse-odds-snapshots 2026-10-04 2026-10-19 43; fill pulse-odds-snapshots 2026-10-15 2026-10-18 0
+ev pulse-odds-snapshots
+check "odds 10-15..10-18 OPEN (real outage)" 'grep -q "data pulse-odds-snapshots: OPEN - 2026-10-15..10-18 missing (0/22 rows)" "$S/logs/ntfy.log"'
+# amazon_creator_connections_daily 2026-10-22: 1 row (real), floor 3
+at "2026-10-25 12:00"; fill amz-creator-connections 2026-10-08 2026-10-23 7; setc amz-creator-connections 2026-10-22 1
+ev amz-creator-connections
+check "cc 10-22 short 1/3 OPEN (real short day)" '[[ "$(gst amz-creator-connections 2026-10-22 status)" == short && "$(gst amz-creator-connections 2026-10-22 rows_before)" == 1 ]]'
+check "cc 10-23 (lag 2, ready 10-25 11:00) judged; 10-24 not" '[[ -z "$(gst amz-creator-connections 2026-10-24 state)" ]] && grep -q "amz-creator-connections 2026-10-23" "$S/counts.log" && ! grep -q "amz-creator-connections 2026-10-24" "$S/counts.log"'
+# pulse_bb_showings_daily: 21/day through 11-08, nothing after (real, found in Phase 0)
+at "2026-11-15 13:30"; fill bb-showings-daily 2026-10-25 2026-11-08 21
+ev bb-showings-daily
+check "bb 11-09..11-14 OPEN (real gap)" 'grep -q "data bb-showings-daily: OPEN - 2026-11-09..11-14 missing (0/10 rows)" "$S/logs/ntfy.log"'
+
+
+echo "D10 supervisor hook: gated on data.enabled; heartbeat lists open gaps only; status has the DATA table"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; setc scratch-daily 2026-11-12 0
+fill scratch-weekday 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 3; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 9; fill scratch-lost 2026-11-01 2026-11-18 4
+for a in 2026-10-19 2026-10-26 2026-11-02 2026-11-09 2026-11-16; do setc scratch-weekly "$a" 500; done
+mkdir -p "$S/registry"
+bash "$ROOT/lib/supervisor.sh" tick > "$S/sup.log" 2>&1
+check "data.enabled absent: the tick does no data work" '[[ ! -d "$S/data/state" ]]'
+touch "$S/data.enabled"
+bash "$ROOT/lib/supervisor.sh" tick > "$S/sup.log" 2>&1
+check "data.enabled: every watched series evaluated (5 state files)" '[[ "$(ls "$S/data/state" | grep -c "^scratch-.*json$")" == 5 ]]'
+check "excluded series never evaluated" '[[ ! -f "$S/data/state/scratch-excluded.json" ]]'
+check "the tick logged the gap transition" 'grep -q "data: scratch-daily: 2026-11-12 -> OPEN" "$S/sup.log"'
+hb=$(WALKAWAY_HEARTBEAT_HOURS="$(TZ=America/New_York date +%H)" bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/supervisor.sh'; supervisor_heartbeat"; tail -1 "$S/logs/ntfy.log")
+check "heartbeat: one line for the series with an open gap, none for complete series" 'grep -q "data scratch-daily: 1 open gap(s) 2026-11-12 OPEN" <<< "$hb" && ! grep -q "data scratch-weekday" <<< "$hb"'
+st=$(bash "$ROOT/walkaway.sh" status 2>&1)
+check "walkaway status prints the DATA table (series, last complete, open gaps, last action)" 'grep -q "^DATA (2026-11-18, data watching on)" <<< "$st" && grep -qE "^SERIES +LAST COMPLETE +OPEN GAPS +LAST ACTION" <<< "$st" && grep -qE "^scratch-daily +2026-11-17 +11-12 OPEN" <<< "$st"'
+
+echo ""
+echo "Results: $PASS passed, $FAIL failed"
+(( FAIL == 0 ))

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Data operator (lib/data-operator.sh) + its hand-off from the data watch (lib/data.sh) in a scratch world: scratch
+# series (tests/fixtures/data/series-test.yaml) with owner repo = a scratch repo, fixture counts, stubbed re-pull
+# triggers, walkaway launches with the stub builder, claude -p replaced by a stub returning canned decisions, ntfy
+# recorded to ntfy.log only. Exit 0 = all pass.
+set -uo pipefail
+
+SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
+ROOT="${SCRIPT_DIR}/.."
+# shellcheck source=tests/drill/scratch.sh
+source "${SCRIPT_DIR}/drill/scratch.sh"
+
+W=$(mktemp -d)
+export WALKAWAY_HOME="$W/home" WALKAWAY_NTFY_DISABLED=1 NTFY_TOPIC=data-op-test WALKAWAY_HEARTBEAT_HOURS=off
+export WALKAWAY_BUILDER_CMD="${SCRIPT_DIR}/drill/stub-builder.sh" WALKAWAY_GH_CMD="${SCRIPT_DIR}/drill/gh-sim.sh" GH_SIM_DIR="$W/gh"
+export WALKAWAY_OPERATOR_CMD="$W/stub-claude" WALKAWAY_LAUNCH_LOG_DIR="$W/logs" WALKAWAY_CLAUDE_PROJECTS="$W/none"
+export WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="$W/series.yaml" WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES="$W/fx" WALKAWAY_DATA_TRIGGER_CMD="$ROOT/tests/fixtures/data/trigger-stub.sh"
+export WALKAWAY_DATA_GUARD_FIXTURE="$W/guard.json"
+mkdir -p "$WALKAWAY_HOME/queue" "$W/logs" "$W/decisions" "$W/fx"
+echo '[]' > "$W/guard.json"
+touch "$WALKAWAY_HOME/supervisor.enabled"
+
+PASS=0
+FAIL=0
+check() { local label="$1"; shift; if "$@"; then echo "  PASS: $label"; PASS=$((PASS + 1)); else echo "  FAIL: $label"; FAIL=$((FAIL + 1)); fi; }
+at() { export WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW; WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW=$(TZ=America/New_York date -j -f '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M' "$1" +%s); }
+fill() {
+  python3 - "$W/fx/$1.json" "$2" "$3" "$4" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, datetime, os
+p, a, b, c = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], int(sys.argv[4])
+d = json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else {}
+x = datetime.date.fromisoformat(a); e = datetime.date.fromisoformat(b)
+while x <= e:
+    d[x.isoformat()] = c; x += datetime.timedelta(days=1)
+json.dump(d, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+}
+gst() { jq -r --arg p "$2" ".gaps[\$p].$3 // empty" "$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/$1.json" 2>/dev/null; }
+sget() { jq -r ".$2 // empty" "$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/$1.json" 2>/dev/null; }
+nt() { local n; n=$(grep -c "data $1: $2 - " "$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log" 2>/dev/null); echo "${n:-0}"; }
+trig() { local n; n=$(grep -c "^$1 " "$WALKAWAY_HOME/triggers.log" 2>/dev/null); echo "${n:-0}"; }
+# ev <series>: one data-watch tick for the series, then wait for a spawned data operator to finish (max 60 s).
+ev() {
+  bash "$ROOT/lib/data.sh" evaluate "$1" >> "$W/data.log" 2>&1
+  local p i
+  p=$(sget "$1" operator_pid)
+  for (( i = 0; i < 60; i++ )); do [[ -n "$p" ]] && kill -0 "$p" 2>/dev/null || break; sleep 1; done
+}
+
+cleanup() {
+  local f p
+  for f in "$W"/home/registry/*.json; do
+    [[ -f "$f" ]] || continue
+    for p in $(jq -r '.builder_pid, .watcher_pid | select(. != null)' "$f"); do pkill -P "$p" 2>/dev/null; kill "$p" 2>/dev/null; done
+  done
+  [[ -n "${KEEP_WORK:-}" ]] && echo "kept $W" || rm -rf "$W"
+}
+trap cleanup EXIT
+
+# claude stub: the decision for the series named on the task's "Series: <name> (" line.
+cat > "$W/stub-claude" <<EOF
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+s=\$(printf '%s\n' "\$2" | sed -n 's/^Series: \([a-z0-9-]*\) (.*/\1/p' | head -1)
+echo "\$(date +%T) stub-claude \$s cwd=\$(pwd)" >> "$W/stub-claude.log"
+jq -c '{structured_output: .}' "$W/decisions/\$s.json"
+EOF
+chmod +x "$W/stub-claude"
+
+# Scratch owner repo; the test series with their owner repo pointed at it (JSON is valid YAML).
+R1=$(drill_make_repo "$W/repos" audrey-scratch)
+drill_set_modes "$R1" fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118 work
+WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="$ROOT/tests/fixtures/data/series-test.yaml" bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/data.sh'; data_config" |
+  jq --arg r "$R1" '.series |= map(.owner_repo = $r)' > "$W/series.yaml"
+
+FIX_DECISION='{"decision":"fix","protected_hit":false,"needs_live_action":false,"confidence":"high",
+ "cause":"No re-pull path covers ages 9-10: run-now pulls yesterday only and the self-heal window ends at today-8 (missing/route.ts eligibleDates). The 11-08/11-09 pulls failed on a logged-in session that later recovered.",
+ "evidence":["counts 2026-11-08=0, 2026-11-09=0 (floor 5)","src/app/api/ingest/amazon/missing/route.ts:41-48 eligibleDates"],
+ "fix":{"title":"Date-range re-pull hook for tracking","goal":"The supervisor can re-pull any tracking date range through the existing heal path.",
+   "what_to_build":["extension/background.js: URL trigger #sr-ext-repull=tracking_offsite&from=..&to=.. -> runReport(customDate) per date"],
+   "acceptance_criteria":[{"id":"AC1","text":"hook pulls exactly 2026-11-08..2026-11-09 in a fixture run","evidence_file":"handoff/fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118-evidence/hook-test.txt"}],
+   "regression_fixture":"counts 11-08=0 / 11-09=0 with no covering method",
+   "files_expected":["extension/background.js","extension/heal-lib.js","tests/heal-hook.test.ts"],
+   "do_not_build":["No change to the scheduled morning pull"],"stop_rules":["Stop if the hook needs a credential"]}}'
+
+echo "O1 lost data: the source cannot re-pull a past day -> ESCALATED (high) naming the lost dates, no model"
+at "2026-11-18 14:00"
+fill scratch-lost 2026-11-01 2026-11-18 4; fill scratch-lost 2026-11-15 2026-11-16 0
+ev scratch-lost
+check "gaps ESCALATED + marked lost" bash -c "[[ \$(jq -r '.gaps[\"2026-11-15\"].state' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/scratch-lost.json') == ESCALATED && \$(jq -r '.gaps[\"2026-11-16\"].lost' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/scratch-lost.json') == true ]]"
+check "one ESCALATED ntfy, priority high, with the dates and the reason" grep -q $'\thigh\tdata scratch-lost: ESCALATED - scratch-lost lost 2026-11-15, 2026-11-16 (0/4 rows): the source cannot re-pull a past day. Accept the loss, or fix the pull so it stops missing days?$' "$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log"
+check "claude not asked (deterministic)" bash -c "! grep -q 'stub-claude scratch-lost' '$W/stub-claude.log' 2>/dev/null"
+check "incident file written" test -f "$WALKAWAY_HOME/data-fixes/scratch-lost-2026-11-18/handoff/job-incident-scratch-lost-2026-11-18.md"
+ev scratch-lost
+check "next tick: nothing new (no re-escalation, no operator)" [ "$(nt scratch-lost ESCALATED)" -eq 1 ]
+
+echo "O2 no re-pull path (historical yes) -> diagnosis -> template-v2 fix plan with the hook contract, launched (FIXING)"
+at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 9; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-08 2026-11-09 0
+echo "$FIX_DECISION" > "$W/decisions/scratch-amazon.json"
+ev scratch-amazon
+WS="$WALKAWAY_HOME/data-fixes/scratch-amazon-2026-11-18/handoff"
+PLAN="$WS/build-plan-fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118.md"
+INC="$WS/job-incident-scratch-amazon-2026-11-18.md"
+check "claude ran read-only in the owner repo" grep -q "stub-claude scratch-amazon cwd=$R1\$" "$W/stub-claude.log"
+check "incident: gaps with counts + reason, cause, fresh counts" bash -c "grep -q '2026-11-08: missing - rows 0 / floor 5' '$INC' && grep -q 'no re-pull path for age 10' '$INC' && grep -q '^## Cause' '$INC' && grep -q 'Fresh read-only counts' '$INC'"
+check "plan has every template-v2 section" bash -c "for s in '## Feature name' '## Branch name' '## Repo' '## Goal' '## Non-goals' '## Evidence' '## Decisions' '## Quality rules' '## Source of truth' '## Acceptance criteria' '## Gate checklist' '## Escalate to Jim' '## Expected files touched' '## Phases' '## Do NOT build' '## Stop rules'; do grep -q \"^\$s\" '$PLAN' || exit 1; done"
+check "plan states the hook contract verbatim + the supervisor's exact call" bash -c "grep -qF 'test hook #sr-ext-repull=tracking_offsite&from=..&to=..' '$PLAN' && grep -qF 'Supervisor call: \`echo fix-hook {from} {to}\`' '$PLAN' && grep -q '^- \[ \] ACH ' '$PLAN'"
+check "plan parses: feature, branch, repo = owner repo" bash -c "[[ \$(bash '$ROOT/lib/parse-plan.sh' '$PLAN' | tr '\n' ' ') == 'FEATURE_NAME=fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118 BRANCH_NAME=feature/fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118 REPO_PATH=$R1 ' ]]"
+check "fix build launched and registered in the owner repo" bash -c "[[ \$(jq -r .repo '$WALKAWAY_HOME/registry/fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118.json') == '$R1' ]]"
+check "gaps FIXING; exactly one OPEN and one FIXING ntfy" bash -c "[[ \$(jq -r '.gaps[\"2026-11-09\"].state' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/scratch-amazon.json') == FIXING ]] && [[ \$(grep -c 'data scratch-amazon: OPEN - ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log') -eq 1 && \$(grep -c 'data scratch-amazon: FIXING - 2026-11-08, 2026-11-09: cause: No re-pull path covers ages 9-10.*fix build fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118 launched' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log') -eq 1 ]]"
+
+echo "O3 fix in flight: ticks follow the build, no trigger, no second plan"
+at "2026-11-18 13:10"; ev scratch-amazon
+check "last action follows the fix build" bash -c "[[ \$(jq -r .last_action '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/scratch-amazon.json') == 'fix build fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118 '* ]]"
+check "no trigger, claude asked once" bash -c "[[ \$(grep -c '^scratch-amazon ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/triggers.log' 2>/dev/null || true) -eq 0 && \$(grep -c 'stub-claude scratch-amazon' '$W/stub-claude.log') -eq 1 ]]"
+
+echo "O4 fix MERGED -> gaps back to OPEN, the hook re-pulls exactly the gap dates, rows land -> CLOSED (one ntfy each)"
+bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/registry.sh'; registry_update fix-data-scratch-amazon-20261118 '.state = \"MERGED\"'"
+export STUB_LAND=9
+at "2026-11-18 14:00"; ev scratch-amazon
+unset STUB_LAND
+check "hook fired once for 2026-11-08..2026-11-09" bash -c "[[ \$(grep -c '^scratch-amazon ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/triggers.log') -eq 1 ]] && grep -q '^scratch-amazon command echo fix-hook {from} {to} 2026-11-08 2026-11-09$' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/triggers.log'"
+check "fix recorded merged + hook enabled" bash -c "[[ \$(jq -r '.fix_merged, .hook_merged' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/scratch-amazon.json' | tr '\n' ' ') == 'true true ' ]]"
+at "2026-11-18 14:02"; ev scratch-amazon
+check "CLOSED rows 0 -> 9 via the fix hook" bash -c "[[ \$(jq -r '.gaps[\"2026-11-08\"].state' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/scratch-amazon.json') == CLOSED ]] && grep -q 'data scratch-amazon: CLOSED - 2026-11-08 rows 0 -> 9, 2026-11-09 rows 0 -> 9 (floor 5; via command (fix hook): echo fix-hook {from} {to})' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log'"
+check "exactly one ntfy per transition: OPEN 1, FIXING 1, CLOSED 1, ESCALATED 0" bash -c "[[ \$(grep -c 'data scratch-amazon: OPEN - ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log') == 1 && \$(grep -c 'data scratch-amazon: FIXING - ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log') == 1 && \$(grep -c 'data scratch-amazon: CLOSED - ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log') == 1 && \$(grep -c 'data scratch-amazon: ESCALATED - ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log') == 0 ]]"
+
+echo "O5 post-fix re-pull fails twice -> ESCALATED (no second fix build, no model)"
+cp "$W/stub-claude.log" "$W/stub-claude.log.before" 2>/dev/null || true
+W3=$(mktemp -d); export WALKAWAY_HOME="$W3/home"; mkdir -p "$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state" "$WALKAWAY_HOME/registry"
+echo '{"feature":"fix-x","state":"MERGED","repo":"/nowhere"}' > "$WALKAWAY_HOME/registry/fix-x.json"
+jq -n '{series: "scratch-amazon", ok_periods: [], fix_feature: "fix-x", fix_merged: false, fix_date: "2026-11-17",
+        gaps: {"2026-11-07": {state: "FIXING", status: "missing", rows_before: 0, rows_now: 0, failed_tries: 2, tries_today: 0}}}' \
+  > "$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/scratch-amazon.json"
+fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-01 2026-11-18 9; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-07 2026-11-07 0
+at "2026-11-18 13:00"; ev scratch-amazon            # fix-x MERGED -> OPEN post_fix -> the hook fires
+check "post-fix: back to OPEN with post_fix, hook fired" bash -c "[[ \$(jq -r '.gaps[\"2026-11-07\"].post_fix' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data/state/scratch-amazon.json') == true ]] && grep -q '^scratch-amazon command echo fix-hook {from} {to} 2026-11-07 2026-11-07$' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/triggers.log'"
+at "2026-11-18 14:01"; ev scratch-amazon            # verify failed -> try 2
+at "2026-11-18 15:02"; ev scratch-amazon            # verify failed -> need_operator -> operator rule 2
+check "two hook triggers, then ESCALATED (high) naming the merged fix" bash -c "[[ \$(grep -c '^scratch-amazon ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/triggers.log') -eq 2 ]] && grep -q \$'\thigh\tdata scratch-amazon: ESCALATED - scratch-amazon 2026-11-07 still 0/5 rows after fix fix-x merged and its hook re-pulled twice' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log'"
+check "no model call, no new plan, no new build" bash -c "diff -q '$W/stub-claude.log' '$W/stub-claude.log.before' >/dev/null && [[ \$(ls '$WALKAWAY_HOME/registry') == fix-x.json ]] && ! ls '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data-fixes/'*/handoff/build-plan-* >/dev/null 2>&1"
+at "2026-11-18 15:04"; ev scratch-amazon
+check "no further trigger or ntfy" bash -c "[[ \$(grep -c '^scratch-amazon ' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/triggers.log') -eq 2 && \$(grep -c 'data scratch-amazon: ESCALATED' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log') -eq 1 ]]"
+rm -rf "$W3"; export WALKAWAY_HOME="$W/home"
+
+echo "O6 the model escalates (protected cause) -> ESCALATED with its one question"
+at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; fill scratch-daily 2026-11-09 2026-11-09 0
+echo '{"decision":"escalate","protected_hit":true,"needs_live_action":true,"confidence":"high","cause":"The pull needs a fresh login.","evidence":["session logged out"],"question":"The scratch pull is logged out since 11-09; can you log in again on the Mini?"}' > "$W/decisions/scratch-daily.json"
+ev scratch-daily
+check "ESCALATED (high) with the model's question" grep -q $'\thigh\tdata scratch-daily: ESCALATED - The scratch pull is logged out since 11-09; can you log in again on the Mini?$' "$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log"
+check "no plan, no build" bash -c "! ls '$WALKAWAY_HOME/data-fixes/scratch-daily-2026-11-18/handoff/'build-plan-* >/dev/null 2>&1 && [[ ! -f '$WALKAWAY_HOME/registry/fix-data-scratch-daily-20261118.json' ]]"
+
+echo "O7 the operator exits without a decision -> ESCALATED once, never a loop"
+W2=$(mktemp -d); export WALKAWAY_HOME="$W2/home"; mkdir -p "$WALKAWAY_HOME"
+printf '#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexit 0\n' > "$W/silent-operator.sh"; chmod +x "$W/silent-operator.sh"
+export WALKAWAY_DATA_OPERATOR="$W/silent-operator.sh"
+at "2026-11-18 13:00"; ev scratch-daily
+at "2026-11-18 13:02"; ev scratch-daily
+at "2026-11-18 13:04"; ev scratch-daily
+check "exactly one ESCALATED 'exited without a decision'" bash -c "[[ \$(grep -c 'data scratch-daily: ESCALATED - Data operator exited without a decision on scratch-daily 2026-11-09' '$WALKAWAY_HOME/logs/ntfy.log') -eq 1 ]]"
+unset WALKAWAY_DATA_OPERATOR; rm -rf "$W2"; export WALKAWAY_HOME="$W/home"
+
+echo ""
+echo "Results: $PASS passed, $FAIL failed"
+(( FAIL == 0 ))

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Re-pull executor tests (lib/data.sh data_act): method choice by age/hours, Amazon guards (business hours, CC batch,
+# pacing), grouped firing, verify -> CLOSED, failed tries, the 2-try cap and the hand-off to the data operator.
+# Triggers are stubbed (tests/fixtures/data/trigger-stub.sh); counts are fixtures; no network.
+# shellcheck disable=SC2034  # variables read inside check's eval strings
+set -uo pipefail
+
+ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
+PASS=0; FAIL=0
+ok()   { PASS=$((PASS + 1)); echo "  ok   $1"; }
+bad()  { FAIL=$((FAIL + 1)); echo "  FAIL $1"; }
+check() { if eval "$2"; then ok "$1"; else bad "$1"; fi; }
+
+export WALKAWAY_NTFY_DISABLED=1 WALKAWAY_HEARTBEAT_HOURS=off
+TEST_YAML="$ROOT/tests/fixtures/data/series-test.yaml"
+STUB="$ROOT/tests/fixtures/data/trigger-stub.sh"
+
+fresh() {
+  S=$(mktemp -d); mkdir -p "$S/fx"
+  export WALKAWAY_HOME="$S" WALKAWAY_DATA_FIXTURES="$S/fx" WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="${1:-$TEST_YAML}" WALKAWAY_DATA_TRIGGER_CMD="$STUB"
+  echo '[]' > "$S/guard.json"; export WALKAWAY_DATA_GUARD_FIXTURE="$S/guard.json" WALKAWAY_DATA_OPERATOR="$ROOT/tests/fixtures/data/operator-stub.sh"
+  unset STUB_LAND STUB_RC
+}
+at() { export WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW; WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW=$(TZ=America/New_York date -j -f '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M' "$1" +%s); }
+fill() {
+  python3 - "$S/fx/$1.json" "$2" "$3" "$4" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, datetime, os
+p, a, b, c = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], int(sys.argv[4])
+d = json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else {}
+x = datetime.date.fromisoformat(a); e = datetime.date.fromisoformat(b)
+while x <= e:
+    d[x.isoformat()] = c; x += datetime.timedelta(days=1)
+json.dump(d, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+}
+ev() { bash "$ROOT/lib/data.sh" evaluate "$1" > /dev/null 2>&1; }
+gst() { jq -r --arg p "$2" ".gaps[\$p].$3 // empty" "$S/data/state/$1.json" 2>/dev/null; }
+ntfy_n() { local n; n=$(grep -c "data $1: $2" "$S/logs/ntfy.log" 2>/dev/null); echo "${n:-0}"; }
+trig_n() { local n; n=$(grep -c "^$1 " "$S/triggers.log" 2>/dev/null); echo "${n:-0}"; }
+
+echo "R1 grouped re-pull fires once (from..to), REPULLING is silent, rows land -> CLOSED with before/after"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; fill scratch-daily 2026-11-15 2026-11-16 0
+export STUB_LAND=12; ev scratch-daily
+check "one trigger for both gaps, 11-15..11-16" '[[ "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "^scratch-daily command true 2026-11-15 2026-11-16$" "$S/triggers.log"'
+check "gaps REPULLING with verify_at = +30 min" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-15 state)" == REPULLING && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-15 verify_at)" == "$((WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW + 1800))" ]]'
+check "REPULLING sends no ntfy (only the OPEN line)" '[[ "$(wc -l < "$S/logs/ntfy.log" | tr -d " ")" == 1 ]]'
+at "2026-11-18 13:02"; ev scratch-daily
+check "rows landed -> CLOSED via the command" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-15 state)" == CLOSED && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 closed_via)" == "command: true" ]]'
+check "one CLOSED ntfy: rows 0 -> 12" '[[ "$(ntfy_n scratch-daily CLOSED)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "CLOSED - 2026-11-15 rows 0 -> 12, 2026-11-16 rows 0 -> 12" "$S/logs/ntfy.log"'
+check "no second trigger" '[[ "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 1 ]]'
+
+echo "R2 re-pull never lands: verify fails twice -> handed to the operator (cap 2), no third trigger"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; fill scratch-daily 2026-11-16 2026-11-16 0
+ev scratch-daily
+at "2026-11-18 13:10"; ev scratch-daily
+check "inside verify window: still REPULLING, no new trigger" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 state)" == REPULLING && "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 1 ]]'
+at "2026-11-18 13:31"; ev scratch-daily
+check "verify failed -> try 2 fired" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 failed_tries)" == 1 && "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 2 && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 state)" == REPULLING ]]'
+at "2026-11-18 14:02"; ev scratch-daily
+check "second failure -> need_operator, reason names the method" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 need_operator)" == true && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 reason)" == "re-pull failed twice (command: true)" ]]'
+at "2026-11-18 15:00"; ev scratch-daily
+check "no third trigger after the cap" '[[ "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 2 ]]'
+
+echo "R3 trigger failure (rc != 0) counts as a failed try"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; fill scratch-daily 2026-11-17 2026-11-17 0
+export STUB_RC=1; ev scratch-daily
+check "OPEN, failed_tries 1, last_try rc 1" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-17 state)" == OPEN && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-17 failed_tries)" == 1 && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-17 last_try.rc)" == 1 ]]'
+at "2026-11-18 13:02"; ev scratch-daily
+check "second failed trigger -> 2 tries" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-17 failed_tries)" == 2 && "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 2 ]]'
+at "2026-11-18 13:04"; ev scratch-daily
+check "-> need_operator" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-17 need_operator)" == true ]]'
+
+echo "R4 Amazon guard: business hours, CC batch in flight, pacing; a deferral is not a try"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 08:30"
+fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 9; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-17 2026-11-17 0
+at "2026-11-18 11:00"; WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW=$(( WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW )) ; at "2026-11-18 19:30"; ev scratch-amazon
+check "19:30: outside 09:00-19:00 -> no trigger, waiting" '[[ "$(trig_n scratch-amazon)" == 0 && "$(gst scratch-amazon 2026-11-17 tries_today)" == 0 ]] && grep -q "outside Amazon business hours 09:00-19:00" <<< "$(jq -r .last_action "$S/data/state/scratch-amazon.json")"'
+fresh; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 9; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-17 2026-11-17 0
+echo '[{"session":"am","status":"RUNNING"}]' > "$S/guard.json"
+at "2026-11-18 11:30"; ev scratch-amazon
+check "CC batch RUNNING -> no trigger, waiting on CC batch" '[[ "$(trig_n scratch-amazon)" == 0 ]] && grep -q "CC batch in flight" <<< "$(jq -r .last_action "$S/data/state/scratch-amazon.json")"'
+echo '[{"session":"am","status":"DONE"}]' > "$S/guard.json"
+at "2026-11-18 11:32"; ev scratch-amazon
+check "batch DONE -> run-now fires (tracking_offsite, age 1)" '[[ "$(trig_n scratch-amazon)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "^scratch-amazon run_now tracking_offsite 2026-11-17 2026-11-17$" "$S/triggers.log"'
+check "pace stamp written" '[[ "$(cat "$S/data/state/_amazon_last_trigger")" == "$WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW" ]]'
+# a second Amazon series inside 15 min waits (real yaml entry: tracking_onsite run-now, same guard)
+export WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="$ROOT/data/series.yaml"; fill amz-tracking-onsite 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 2; fill amz-tracking-onsite 2026-11-17 2026-11-17 0
+at "2026-11-18 11:40"; ev amz-tracking-onsite
+check "amz-tracking-onsite 8 min later: paced, no trigger" '[[ "$(trig_n amz-tracking-onsite)" == 0 ]] && grep -q "paced: last Amazon trigger 8m ago" <<< "$(jq -r .last_action "$S/data/state/amz-tracking-onsite.json")"'
+at "2026-11-18 11:48"; ev amz-tracking-onsite
+check "16 min later: fires run-now tracking_onsite" '[[ "$(trig_n amz-tracking-onsite)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "^amz-tracking-onsite run_now tracking_onsite 2026-11-17" "$S/triggers.log"'
+
+echo "R5 hours windows (real cc entry): run-now pulls D-2 only before noon; after noon age 2 has no path"
+fresh "$ROOT/data/series.yaml"
+fill amz-creator-connections 2026-11-01 2026-11-16 7; fill amz-creator-connections 2026-11-16 2026-11-16 0
+at "2026-11-18 11:30"; ev amz-creator-connections
+check "11:30: run-now cc fires for 11-16 (age 2)" '[[ "$(trig_n amz-creator-connections)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "^amz-creator-connections run_now cc 2026-11-16 2026-11-16$" "$S/triggers.log"'
+fresh "$ROOT/data/series.yaml"
+fill amz-creator-connections 2026-11-01 2026-11-16 7; fill amz-creator-connections 2026-11-16 2026-11-16 0
+at "2026-11-18 12:30"; ev amz-creator-connections
+check "12:30: no method for age 2 -> need_operator, no trigger" '[[ "$(trig_n amz-creator-connections)" == 0 && "$(gst amz-creator-connections 2026-11-16 need_operator)" == true && "$(gst amz-creator-connections 2026-11-16 reason)" == "no re-pull path for age 2" ]]'
+
+echo "R6 self-heal: nothing fires, the wait is the try"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 9; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-15 2026-11-15 0
+ev scratch-amazon
+check "11-15 (age 3) REPULLING via self-heal, verify +180 min" '[[ "$(gst scratch-amazon 2026-11-15 state)" == REPULLING && "$(gst scratch-amazon 2026-11-15 last_try.label)" == "self-heal wait" && "$(gst scratch-amazon 2026-11-15 verify_at)" == "$((WALKAWAY_DATA_NOW + 10800))" ]]'
+fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-15 2026-11-15 9
+at "2026-11-18 15:00"; ev scratch-amazon
+check "extension healed it -> CLOSED via self-heal wait" '[[ "$(gst scratch-amazon 2026-11-15 state)" == CLOSED && "$(gst scratch-amazon 2026-11-15 closed_via)" == "self-heal wait" ]]'
+
+echo "R7 no path / lost data: age beyond every method, historical=no"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; fill scratch-daily 2026-11-08 2026-11-08 0
+ev scratch-daily
+check "age 10 (methods cover 1-3) -> need_operator, no trigger" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-08 need_operator)" == true && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-08 reason)" == "no re-pull path for age 10" && "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 0 ]]'
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 14:00"
+fill scratch-lost 2026-11-01 2026-11-18 4; fill scratch-lost 2026-11-16 2026-11-16 0
+ev scratch-lost
+check "historical=no -> reason says the source cannot re-pull" '[[ "$(gst scratch-lost 2026-11-16 reason)" == "no re-pull path for age 2 (source cannot re-pull a past period)" ]]'
+
+echo "R8 requires_fix hook: unused before the fix merged, used after (dates substituted)"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 9; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-08 2026-11-09 0
+ev scratch-amazon
+check "before the fix: no path (ages 9-10)" '[[ "$(gst scratch-amazon 2026-11-08 need_operator)" == true && "$(trig_n scratch-amazon)" == 0 ]]'
+jq '.hook_merged = true | .gaps |= map_values(del(.need_operator) | .failed_tries = 0)' "$S/data/state/scratch-amazon.json" > "$S/t" && mv "$S/t" "$S/data/state/scratch-amazon.json"
+at "2026-11-18 13:02"; ev scratch-amazon
+check "after the fix merged: the hook fires once for 11-08..11-09" '[[ "$(trig_n scratch-amazon)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "^scratch-amazon command echo fix-hook {from} {to} 2026-11-08 2026-11-09$" "$S/triggers.log"'
+
+echo "R9 run-now allow-list (direct, no network)"
+fresh
+# shellcheck source=lib/data.sh
+out=$(WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="$ROOT/data/series.yaml" bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/data.sh'; cfg=\$(data_config); _run_now \"\$cfg\" \"\$(data_get \"\$cfg\" amz-tracking-offsite)\" cc_optin_runs; echo rc=\$?")
+check "a non-allow-listed report type is refused before any request" '[[ "$out" == *"rc=2"* ]] && grep -q "run_now refused: cc_optin_runs not allow-listed" "$S/logs/data-repull.log"'
+
+echo "R10 a post_fix gap tries the fix hook before the older path (which already failed it)"
+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 9; fill scratch-amazon 2026-11-16 2026-11-16 0
+mkdir -p "$S/data/state"
+jq -n '{series: "scratch-amazon", ok_periods: [], hook_merged: true, fix_merged: true,
+        gaps: {"2026-11-16": {state: "OPEN", status: "missing", rows_before: 0, rows_now: 0, failed_tries: 0, tries_today: 0, post_fix: true}}}' > "$S/data/state/scratch-amazon.json"
+ev scratch-amazon
+check "age 2 (self-heal covers it) but post_fix -> the fix hook fires" '[[ "$(trig_n scratch-amazon)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "^scratch-amazon command echo fix-hook {from} {to} 2026-11-16 2026-11-16$" "$S/triggers.log"'
+
+echo "R11 http trigger (Phase 6 live finding): a route that never answers in time = delivered; no connection = failed try"
+fresh
+printf 'Z=test-cron-secret\n' > "$S/env"
+PORT=$(python3 -c 'import socket; s=socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)); print(s.getsockname()[1]); s.close()')
+python3 - "$PORT" > /dev/null 2>&1 <<'PY' &
+import http.server, sys, time
+class H(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+    def do_GET(self):
+        time.sleep(5)   # a cold start / long sync: answers after the client timeout
+        self.send_response(200); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(b"{}")
+    def log_message(self, *a): pass
+http.server.HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", int(sys.argv[1])), H).serve_forever()
+PY
+SRV=$!
+sleep 1
+CFG=$(jq -cn --arg env "$S/env" --arg url "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT" '{sources: {scratch: {env_file: $env, url_var: "X", key_var: "Y", app_url: $url, cron_secret_var: "Z"}}}')
+SER='{"name":"scratch-http","source":"scratch"}'
+slow=$(WALKAWAY_DATA_HTTP_TIMEOUT=2 bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/data.sh'; _http_get '$CFG' '$SER' /api/cron/x; echo rc=\$?")
+kill "$SRV" 2>/dev/null; wait "$SRV" 2>/dev/null
+refused=$(WALKAWAY_DATA_HTTP_TIMEOUT=2 bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/data.sh'; _http_get '$CFG' '$SER' /api/cron/x; echo rc=\$?")
+check "slow route past the client timeout (curl exit 28) -> rc 0 (delivered; verify decides)" '[[ "$slow" == *"rc=0"* ]] && grep -q "(curl exit 28)" "$S/logs/data-repull.log"'
+check "connection refused (HTTP 000, curl exit 7) -> rc 1 (a failed try, not a fired re-pull)" '[[ "$refused" == *"rc=1"* ]] && grep -q "(curl exit 7)" "$S/logs/data-repull.log"'
+
+echo "R12 a re-pull never fires a send: the ST URL trigger waits until today's Pulse brief row exists (real bb entry)"
+fresh "$ROOT/data/series.yaml"
+fill bb-showings-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-16 21; fill bb-showings-daily 2026-11-17 2026-11-17 0
+echo '[]' > "$S/guard.st_brief_sent.json"
+at "2026-11-18 13:05"; ev bb-showings-daily
+check "brief not sent yet -> no trigger, waiting with the reason" '[[ "$(trig_n bb-showings-daily)" == 0 ]] && grep -q "Pulse brief not sent yet" <<< "$(jq -r .last_action "$S/data/state/bb-showings-daily.json")" && [[ "$(gst bb-showings-daily 2026-11-17 tries_today)" == 0 ]]'
+echo '[{"send_date":"2026-11-18"}]' > "$S/guard.st_brief_sent.json"
+at "2026-11-18 13:07"; ev bb-showings-daily
+check "brief sent -> the ST URL trigger fires for 11-17 (age 1)" '[[ "$(trig_n bb-showings-daily)" == 1 ]] && grep -q "^bb-showings-daily command open -g -a \"Google Chrome\" \"https://throughlinepro.com/#tl-ext-run-daily\" 2026-11-17 2026-11-17$" "$S/triggers.log"'
+
+# R13-R17: Codex cycle 1 regressions (P1 executable fix hooks with full date ranges, P2 verify needs a good count).
+# Real series.yaml entries run with stubbed triggers and a stubbed data operator: nothing live is called.
+
+# all_ok <series> <gap-key>: fixture with every day 2026-09-01..2026-11-30 (so every daily period and Monday
+# anchor) at the series' floor, and <gap-key> = 0
+all_ok() {
+  python3 - "$S/fx/$1.json" "$(jq -r --arg n "$1" '.series[] | select(.name == $n) | .floor' <<< "$CFG_REAL")" "$2" <<'PY'
+import json, sys, datetime
+p, f, g = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
+x = datetime.date(2026, 9, 1); d = {}
+while x <= datetime.date(2026, 11, 30):
+    d[x.isoformat()] = f; x += datetime.timedelta(days=1)
+d[g] = 0
+json.dump(d, open(p, "w"))
+PY
+}
+seed() { mkdir -p "$S/data/state"; jq -n --arg s "$1" --argjson m "$2" '{series: $s, gaps: {}, ok_periods: [], hook_merged: $m}' > "$S/data/state/$1.json"; }
+CFG_REAL=$(WALKAWAY_DATA_FILE="$ROOT/data/series.yaml" bash -c "source '$ROOT/lib/data.sh'; data_config")
+
+echo "R13 (a) series.yaml contract: every re-pullable watched series has a fix_hook AND a matching executable requires_fix method with {from}/{to}"
+viol=$(jq -r '.series[] | select(.watch == true) | . as $s
+  | [(.repull // [])[] | select(.requires_fix == true)] as $fx
+  | if (.historical == "no") then
+      (if (.fix_hook // "") != "" or ($fx | length) > 0 then "\(.name): lost-data series must keep no hook" else empty end)
+    else
+      (if (.fix_hook // "") == "" then "\(.name): no fix_hook" else empty end),
+      (if ($fx | length) == 0 then "\(.name): no requires_fix method" else empty end),
+      ($fx[] | (.command // .path // "") as $t
+        | (if ($t | contains("{from}") and contains("{to}")) | not then "\($s.name): requires_fix trigger lacks {from}/{to}: \($t)" else empty end),
+          (if .ages != "all" then "\($s.name): requires_fix method must cover ages all" else empty end),
+          (if .method == "http" then ($t | split("?")[0]) else ($t | capture("(?<k>#(sr|tl)-ext-repull=[a-z_]+)").k // "") end) as $k
+          | if $k == "" or (($s.fix_hook // "") | contains($k) | not) then "\($s.name): requires_fix trigger \($k) not in its fix_hook" else empty end)
+    end' <<< "$CFG_REAL")

+check "the 10 series named by Codex cycle 1 each have a requires_fix method" '[[ "$(jq -r "[.series[] | select(.name | IN(\"amz-connect-summary\",\"markable-spend\",\"meta-daily-metrics\",\"pulse-daily-snapshots\",\"pulse-weekly-stats\",\"pulse-signal-scores\",\"pulse-market-health\",\"tma-cohort-showings\",\"recap-reports\",\"pulse-showings-weekly\")) | select([(.repull // [])[] | select(.requires_fix)] | length > 0)] | length" <<< "$CFG_REAL")" == 10 ]]'
+check "pulse-signal-scores / pulse-market-health hooks never use ?scope=full" '! jq -r ".series[] | (.repull // [])[] | .path // empty" <<< "$CFG_REAL" | grep -q "scope=full"'
+
+echo "R14 (b) pulse-weekly-stats week gap, hook merged: the hook gets from = Monday, to = Sunday"
+fresh "$ROOT/data/series.yaml"; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+all_ok pulse-weekly-stats 2026-11-02; seed pulse-weekly-stats true
+ev pulse-weekly-stats
+check "fired once for week 202645: 2026-11-02..2026-11-08" '[[ "$(trig_n pulse-weekly-stats)" == 1 ]] && grep -q " 2026-11-02 2026-11-08$" "$S/triggers.log"'
+check "resolved path /api/cron/pulse-daily-sync?from=2026-11-02&to=2026-11-08" 'grep -qxF "pulse-weekly-stats http /api/cron/pulse-daily-sync?from=2026-11-02&to=2026-11-08" "$S/triggers-resolved.log"'
+check "gap REPULLING via the fix hook" '[[ "$(gst pulse-weekly-stats 2026-11-02 state)" == REPULLING ]]'
+

+fresh "$ROOT/data/series.yaml"; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+all_ok meta-daily-metrics 2026-11-08; seed meta-daily-metrics true
+ev meta-daily-metrics
+check "age 10 (meta-sync covers 1-3) -> the fix hook fires with 11-08..11-08" 'grep -qxF "meta-daily-metrics http /api/cron/meta-sync?from=2026-11-08&to=2026-11-08" "$S/triggers-resolved.log"'
+fresh "$ROOT/data/series.yaml"; unset WALKAWAY_DATA_TRIGGER_CMD
+printf 'CRON_SECRET=test-cron-secret\n' > "$S/env"

+import http.server, sys
+class H(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+    def do_GET(self):

+SRV=$!
+sleep 1
+CFG_LOCAL=$(jq -c --arg env "$S/env" --arg url "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT" '.sources.audrey += {env_file: $env, app_url: $url}' <<< "$CFG_REAL")
+IDX=$(jq -r '.series[] | select(.name == "meta-daily-metrics") | .repull | to_entries[] | select(.value.requires_fix) | .key' <<< "$CFG_REAL")
+# the config travels in the environment (its notes hold quotes)
+fire_direct() { CFG_LOCAL="$CFG_LOCAL" IDX="$IDX" F="$1" T="$2" bash -c 'source "$0/lib/data.sh"; s=$(data_get "$CFG_LOCAL" meta-daily-metrics); _fire "$CFG_LOCAL" "$s" "$IDX" "$F" "$T"; echo rc=$?' "$ROOT"; }
+fired=$(fire_direct 2026-11-08 2026-11-09)

+

+fresh; at "2026-11-18 13:00"
+fill scratch-daily 2026-11-01 2026-11-17 12; fill scratch-daily 2026-11-16 2026-11-16 0
+ev scratch-daily
+cp "$S/fx/scratch-daily.json" "$S/fx-good.json"; echo '{"__error": "HTTP 503 (fixture outage)"}' > "$S/fx/scratch-daily.json"
+at "2026-11-18 13:31"; ev scratch-daily
+check "errored count at verify_at: failed_tries 0, still REPULLING, no new trigger" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 failed_tries)" == 0 && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 state)" == REPULLING && "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 1 ]]'

+check "next good count (0 < floor 10): exactly one failed try, try 2 fired" '[[ "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 failed_tries)" == 1 && "$(trig_n scratch-daily)" == 2 && "$(gst scratch-daily 2026-11-16 state)" == REPULLING ]]'
+
+echo "R17 (e) a requires_fix method NEVER fires while hook_merged is false (every real series with one); fires once merged"
+R17_N=0
+while IFS=$'\t' read -r name cad tmpl; do
+  fresh "$ROOT/data/series.yaml"; at "2026-11-18 13:30"
+  # the oldest ready lookback period: the age least covered by the existing paths

+  check "$name: hook not merged -> the fix hook is not called" '! grep -qsF -- "$tmpl" "$S/triggers.log"'

+    check "$name: no other path for $gap -> handed to the data operator, nothing fired" '[[ "$(trig_n "$name")" == 0 && "$(gst "$name" "$gap" reason)" == "no re-pull path for age "* ]]'
+    jq '.hook_merged = true | del(.operator_gaps) | .gaps |= map_values(del(.need_operator) | .failed_tries = 0)' "$S/data/state/$name.json" > "$S/t" && mv "$S/t" "$S/data/state/$name.json"
+    at "2026-11-18 13:50"; ev "$name"
+    check "$name: hook merged -> the fix hook fires once for $gap" '[[ "$(grep -cF -- "$tmpl" "$S/triggers.log")" == 1 ]]'
+  fi
+done < <(jq -r '.series[] | select(.watch == true) | . as $s | (.repull // [])[] | select(.requires_fix == true)
+          | [$s.name, $s.cadence, (.command // .path)] | @tsv' <<< "$CFG_REAL")
+check "R17 covered all 16 re-pullable watched series" '[[ "$R17_N" == 16 ]]'
+
+echo ""
+echo "Results: $PASS passed, $FAIL failed"
+(( FAIL == 0 ))
